## Day 21: Regular Past Simple Verbs (-ed, -d, -ied) & Yesterday's Actions {#day-21-day_21}
**शीर्षक**: नियमित भूतकाल क्रियाएँ (-ed, -d, -ied) और कल के कार्य  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.2` | **Est. Duration**: 30 mins | **Prerequisites**: Day 06 (Was/Were Past State), Day 11–12 (Present Action Verbs)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Form affirmative past simple sentences using regular verbs with '-ed', '-d', and '-ied' endings.
  - **HI**: नियमित क्रियाओं (Regular Verbs) में '-ed', '-d' और '-ied' जोड़कर भूतकाल के सकारात्मक वाक्य बनाना।
- **EN**: Master the 3 phonetic pronunciation rules for '-ed' endings: /t/ (walked), /d/ (played), and /ɪd/ (waited).
  - **HI**: '-ed' के 3 अलग-अलग उच्चारण नियमों को समझना: /t/ (walked), /d/ (played) और /ɪd/ (waited)।
- **EN**: Describe completed actions using past time expressions like 'yesterday', 'last night', and 'yesterday morning'.
  - **HI**: 'Yesterday', 'last night' जैसे समय संकेतकों के साथ कल घटी घटनाओं का वर्णन करना।
- **EN**: Eliminate the common L1 error of using present tense or omitting past inflections when narrating yesterday's events.
  - **HI**: हिन्दी भाषियों द्वारा भूतकाल की बात करते समय क्रिया के वर्तमान रूप का प्रयोग करने की गलती से बचना।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
जब हम भूतकाल में किसी निश्चित समय पर पूरे हो चुके काम की बात करते हैं (जैसे 'मैंने कल काम किया', 'उसने मुझे फ़ोन किया'), तो हम अंग्रेज़ी में 'Past Simple' (भूतकाल) का प्रयोग करते हैं। नियमित क्रियाओं (Regular Verbs) के लिए हम क्रिया के मूल रूप (V1) में '-ed' या '-d' जोड़ते हैं, जिससे वह Past Form (V2) बन जाती है। सबसे अच्छी बात यह है कि Past Simple के सकारात्मक वाक्यों में सभी कर्ताओं (I, You, We, They, He, She, It) के साथ क्रिया का वही दूसरा रूप (V2) आता है, इसमें 's/es' का कोई झंझट नहीं होता!

**English Explanation**:
The Past Simple tense is used to describe completed actions that took place at a specific time in the past. For regular verbs, we form the past simple (V2) by adding '-ed' (work -> worked), '-d' (live -> lived), or '-ied' (study -> studied). Unlike the present simple, the verb form remains identical across all subjects (I, you, he, she, we, they). A crucial aspect of mastery is the phonetics of '-ed': it is pronounced as /t/ after voiceless sounds, /d/ after voiced sounds, and /ɪd/ only after 't' or 'd' endings.

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: Uniform Past Simple Invariable Rule** [Core Syntax Standard]
  The regular past tense form (V2) is identical for all subjects (I worked, you worked, she worked, they worked). There is no third-person singular '-s' in the past simple.
- **Rule 2: Spelling Rules for Regular Past Verbs** [Orthographic Rule]
  1. Most verbs add '-ed' (clean -> cleaned). 2. Verbs ending in '-e' add only '-d' (decide -> decided). 3. Consonant + 'y' changes to '-ied' (study -> studied). 4. Short vowel + consonant doubles the final consonant (stop -> stopped, plan -> planned).
- **Rule 3: Three Phonetic Pronunciations of '-ed'** [Phonological Standard]
  /ɪd/ (extra syllable) ONLY after /t/ or /d/ (waited, needed). /t/ after voiceless sounds /p, k, f, s, ʃ, tʃ/ (helped, looked, washed). /d/ after all other voiced sounds and vowels (played, cleaned, called).
- **Rule 4: Specific Past Time Anchor Requirement** [Pragmatic Usage]
  Past simple clauses are typically anchored by specific completed time markers: yesterday, last night, last week, in 2022, two hours ago.

#### Master Formula Table:
```json
[
  {
    "subjectGroup": "All Subjects (I / You / He / She / We / They)",
    "formula": "Subject + Verb-ed (V2) + [Object] + [Past Time Marker]",
    "exampleEn": "I cleaned my room yesterday.",
    "meaningHi": "मैंने कल अपना कमरा साफ़ किया।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Verbs ending in -e",
    "formula": "Subject + Verb-d (V2) + [Complement]",
    "exampleEn": "She lived in Pune for two years.",
    "meaningHi": "वह दो साल तक पुणे में रही।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Verbs ending in Consonant + y",
    "formula": "Subject + Verb-ied (V2) + [Object]",
    "exampleEn": "Rohan studied all night.",
    "meaningHi": "रोहन ने पूरी रात पढ़ाई की।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "CVC Doubling Verbs",
    "formula": "Subject + Doubled Consonant + ed (V2)",
    "exampleEn": "The bus stopped suddenly.",
    "meaningHi": "बस अचानक रुक गई।"
  }
]
```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: Over-pronouncing '-ed' as a separate syllable and failing to mark V2 in speech
- **Hindi Pattern**: भारतीय शिक्षार्थी अक्सर 'worked' को 'वर्क-एड' (/wɜːrk-ed/) बोलते हैं, जबकि इसे सिर्फ /wɜːrkt/ बोलना चाहिए। साथ ही बातचीत में 'कल' बोलने के बाद भी क्रिया में V2 लगाना भूल जाते हैं।
- **Potential Interference**: `Saying '*Yesterday I work*' instead of '*Yesterday I worked*', or pronouncing *helped* as '*help-ed*'.`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: '-ed' is only pronounced as an extra syllable /ɪd/ when the root verb ends in 't' or 'd' (e.g. want -> wanted). In all other verbs, it is a single syllable (/t/ or /d/).
- **Natural English Solution**: **Always use V2 for completed past actions and pronounce 'worked' as /wɜːrkt/ and 'played' as /pleɪd/.**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day21_vocab_001` | **Worked** | Verb (V2) | `/wɜːkt/` | काम किया | Priya worked late yesterday evening. <br> *प्रिया ने कल शाम देर तक काम किया।* |
| `day21_vocab_002` | **Visited** | Verb (V2) | `/ˈvɪz.ɪ.tɪd/` | मुलाक़ात की / दौरा किया | We visited our grandparents last Sunday. <br> *हमने पिछले रविवार अपने दादा-दादी से मुलाक़ात की।* |
| `day21_vocab_003` | **Cleaned** | Verb (V2) | `/kliːnd/` | साफ़ किया | He cleaned the entire kitchen this morning. <br> *उसने आज सुबह पूरा किचन साफ़ किया।* |
| `day21_vocab_004` | **Finished** | Verb (V2) | `/ˈfɪn.ɪʃt/` | समाप्त किया / पूरा किया | I finished my project on time. <br> *मैंने अपना प्रोजेक्ट समय पर पूरा किया।* |
| `day21_vocab_005` | **Watched** | Verb (V2) | `/wɒtʃt/` | देखा (स्क्रीन / मैच) | They watched a cricket match last night. <br> *उन्होंने कल रात एक क्रिकेट मैच देखा।* |
| `day21_vocab_006` | **Cooked** | Verb (V2) | `/kʊkt/` | खाना पकाया | My brother cooked delicious biryani. <br> *मेरे भाई ने स्वादिष्ट बिरयानी बनाई।* |
| `day21_vocab_007` | **Waited** | Verb (V2) | `/ˈweɪ.tɪd/` | इंतज़ार किया | We waited for the bus for forty minutes. <br> *हमने चालीस मिनट तक बस का इंतज़ार किया।* |
| `day21_vocab_008` | **Called** | Verb (V2) | `/kɔːld/` | फ़ोन किया / बुलाया | She called me yesterday afternoon. <br> *उसने कल दोपहर मुझे फ़ोन किया।* |
| `day21_vocab_009` | **Decided** | Verb (V2) | `/dɪˈsaɪ.dɪd/` | निर्णय लिया / तय किया | They decided to buy a new laptop. <br> *उन्होंने एक नया लैपटॉप खरीदने का फ़ैसला किया।* |
| `day21_vocab_010` | **Started** | Verb (V2) | `/ˈstɑː.tɪd/` | शुरू किया | The meeting started at nine sharp. <br> *मीटिंग ठीक नौ बजे शुरू हुई।* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day21_phrase_001` [Time Expression]: **"Yesterday morning"** — *कल सुबह*
- `day21_phrase_002` [Time Expression]: **"Last night"** — *कल रात*
- `day21_phrase_003` [Conversational]: **"I worked all day."** — *मैंने पूरे दिन काम किया।*
- `day21_phrase_004` [Conversational]: **"She called me back."** — *उसने मुझे वापस फ़ोन किया।*
- `day21_phrase_005` [Conversational]: **"We stayed at home."** — *हम घर पर ही रुके।*
- `day21_phrase_006` [Conversational]: **"It ended very late."** — *यह बहुत देर से समाप्त हुआ।*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day21_example_001`: **I walked to the office yesterday.**  
  *मैं कल पैदल दफ़्तर गया।*
- `day21_example_002`: **She waited for the train for two hours.**  
  *उसने दो घंटे तक ट्रेन का इंतज़ार किया।*
- `day21_example_003`: **We enjoyed the movie last night.**  
  *हमने कल रात फ़िल्म का आनंद लिया।*
- `day21_example_004`: **Ravi called his manager in the morning.**  
  *रवि ने सुबह अपने मैनेजर को फ़ोन किया।*
- `day21_example_005`: **They lived in Mumbai five years ago.**  
  *वे पाँच साल पहले मुंबई में रहते थे।*
- `day21_example_006`: **I cooked dinner for my family.**  
  *मैंने अपने परिवार के लिए रात का खाना बनाया।*
- `day21_example_007`: **The rain stopped an hour ago.**  
  *बारिश एक घंटे पहले रुक गई।*
- `day21_example_008`: **He studied hard for his exam.**  
  *उसने अपनी परीक्षा के लिए कड़ी मेहनत से पढ़ाई की।*
- `day21_example_009`: **My sister helped me with my homework.**  
  *मेरी बहन ने मेरे होमवर्क में मेरी मदद की।*
- `day21_example_010`: **We arrived at the airport on time.**  
  *हम समय पर हवाई अड्डे पहुँचे।*
- `day21_example_011`: **The shop closed at eight o'clock.**  
  *दुकान आठ बजे बंद हो गई।*
- `day21_example_012`: **I washed my car yesterday afternoon.**  
  *मैंने कल दोपहर अपनी कार धोई।*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Voiceless ending /t/ in 'worked', 'watched', 'cooked', Voiced ending /d/ in 'played', 'cleaned', 'called', Syllabic /ɪd/ in 'waited', 'decided', 'started'
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: Do NOT pronounce '-ed' as a separate syllable /ed/ unless the verb ends with a 'T' or 'D' sound. 'Cooked' is /kʊkt/ (1 syllable), NOT 'cook-ed' (2 syllables).
#### Audio Drills:
- `day21_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "I worked, watched, and cooked yesterday."
- `day21_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "They played, cleaned, and called their friends."
- `day21_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "We waited, decided, and started the task."
- `day21_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "She visited the doctor and rested at home."

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (35 Items)
#### 1. `[day21_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `REGULAR_PAST_AFFIRMATIVE`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct sentence to say 'मैंने कल रात फ़िल्म देखी':
**Options**: `I watch movie last night.` | `I watched a movie last night.` | `I watching movie last night.` | `I was watch a movie last night.`
- **Correct Answer**: `I watched a movie last night.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Past completed action requires the V2 regular verb 'watched' with 'last night'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: कल रात की पूर्ण क्रिया के लिए V2 'watched' का प्रयोग होगा।

#### 2. `[day21_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PAST_SPELLING`)
**Prompt**: What is the correct past simple spelling of the verb 'Study'?
**Options**: `Studyed` | `Studied` | `Studyied` | `Studying`
- **Correct Answer**: `Studied`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Consonant + 'y' changes to '-ied' in regular past tense.
- **Explanation (HI)**: व्यंजन + 'y' पर समाप्त होने वाली क्रियाओं में 'y' हटाकर '-ied' जुड़ता है।

#### 3. `[day21_ex_003]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (medium | `ED_PRONUNCIATION`)
**Prompt**: Which verb's '-ed' ending is pronounced with an extra syllable /ɪd/?
**Options**: `Worked` | `Cleaned` | `Waited` | `Cooked`
- **Correct Answer**: `Waited`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Verbs ending in /t/ or /d/ sounds take the /ɪd/ pronunciation (e.g. wait -> waited).
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Wait' का अंत /t/ ध्वनि पर होता है, इसलिए इसका '-ed' /ɪd/ की तरह उच्चारित होता है।

#### 4. `[day21_ex_004]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `REGULAR_PAST_AFFIRMATIVE`)
**Prompt**: Complete the sentence: 'Priya ______ (call) her mother yesterday afternoon.'
- **Correct Answer**: `called`
- **Explanation (EN)**: The past simple form of 'call' is 'called'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Call' का Past Simple रूप 'called' है।

#### 5. `[day21_ex_005]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (medium | `PAST_SPELLING`)
**Prompt**: Complete the sentence: 'The bus ______ (stop) in front of the hospital.'
- **Correct Answer**: `stopped`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Stop' has a short vowel + consonant pattern, so the final 'p' is doubled: 'stopped'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Stop' में अंतिम अक्षर डबल होकर 'stopped' बनता है।

#### 6. `[day21_ex_006]` [WORD_REORDER] (medium | `SENTENCE_BUILDING`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a correct sentence: [yesterday / the room / cleaned / Amit / evening]
**Options**: `Amit cleaned the room yesterday evening.` | `Cleaned Amit the room yesterday evening.` | `Yesterday evening Amit room the cleaned.` | `Amit yesterday evening room cleaned the.`
- **Correct Answer**: `Amit cleaned the room yesterday evening.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Standard word order: Subject (Amit) + V2 (cleaned) + Object (the room) + Time (yesterday evening).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: कर्ता (Amit) + क्रिया V2 (cleaned) + कर्म (the room) + समय (yesterday evening)।

#### 7. `[day21_ex_007]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `ERROR_DETECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the mistake: 'Yesterday I walk to school because the bus was late.'
- **Correct Answer**: `Yesterday I walked to school because the bus was late.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Walk' must be in past tense 'walked' to match 'yesterday'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Yesterday' के कारण 'walk' का रूप बदलकर 'walked' होगा।

#### 8. `[day21_ex_008]` [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (easy | `TRANSLATION_HI_EN`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'हमने कल रात स्वादिष्ट खाना पकाया।'
**Options**: `We cooked delicious food last night.` | `We cook delicious food last night.` | `We are cook delicious food last night.` | `We were cooked delicious food last night.`
- **Correct Answer**: `We cooked delicious food last night.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'हमने पकाया' = 'We cooked', 'कल रात' = 'last night'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अनुवाद: 'We cooked delicious food last night.'

#### 9. `[day21_ex_009]` [ENGLISH_TO_HINDI] (easy | `TRANSLATION_EN_HI`)
**Prompt**: What is the Hindi meaning of: 'They decided to stay at home yesterday'?
**Options**: `वे कल घर पर रहने का निर्णय ले रहे थे।` | `उन्होंने कल घर पर ही रुकने का फ़ैसला किया।` | `वे कल घर पर नहीं रुके।` | `उन्होंने घर साफ़ करने का फ़ैसला किया।`
- **Correct Answer**: `उन्होंने कल घर पर ही रुकने का फ़ैसला किया।`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Decided' means 'फ़ैसला किया', 'stay at home' means 'घर पर रुकना'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अर्थ: 'उन्होंने कल घर पर ही रुकने का फ़ैसला किया।'

#### 10. `[day21_ex_010]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PAST_SPELLING`)
**Prompt**: Which pair shows the correct past simple spelling?
**Options**: `Plan -> Planed` | `Live -> Lived` | `Try -> Tryed` | `Decide -> Decidied`
- **Correct Answer**: `Live -> Lived`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Verbs ending in '-e' just add '-d': Live -> Lived.
- **Explanation (HI)**: '-e' पर समाप्त होने वाले शब्दों में सिर्फ '-d' जुड़ता है।

#### 11. `[day21_ex_011]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (medium | `ED_PRONUNCIATION`)
**Prompt**: How many syllables are pronounced in the word 'WORKED'?
**Options**: `1 syllable (/wɜːkt/)` | `2 syllables (/wɜːk-ed/)` | `3 syllables` | `None`
- **Correct Answer**: `1 syllable (/wɜːkt/)`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Worked' is pronounced as a single syllable /wɜːkt/ because /k/ is voiceless.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Worked' सिर्फ एक सिलेबल में /wɜːkt/ बोला जाता है, 'वर्क-एड' नहीं।

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: Two colleagues chatting in the office cafeteria on Monday morning about their productive Sunday tasks.

#### Dialogue (`day21_dialogue_001`):
- **Amit**: "Good morning, Priya! Did you relax yesterday?"  
  *(शुभ प्रभात, प्रिया! क्या तुमने कल आराम किया?)*
- **Priya**: "Not really, Amit. I cleaned my entire apartment and washed all my clothes yesterday morning."  
  *(ज़्यादा नहीं, अमित। मैंने कल सुबह अपना पूरा अपार्टमेंट साफ़ किया और सारे कपड़े धोए।)*
- **Amit**: "That sounds like hard work! Did you cook lunch at home?"  
  *(यह तो काफी मेहनत भरा काम लगता है! क्या तुमने घर पर दोपहर का खाना बनाया?)*
- **Priya**: "Yes, I cooked chicken biryani, and in the evening my cousin visited me. We watched a comedy movie together."  
  *(हाँ, मैंने चिकन बिरयानी बनाई, और शाम को मेरा चचेरा भाई मुझसे मिलने आया। हमने साथ में एक कॉमेडी फ़िल्म देखी।)*
- **Amit**: "That sounds like a wonderful weekend! I stayed at home and rested all day."  
  *(यह तो बहुत बढ़िया सप्ताहांत रहा! मैं पूरे दिन घर पर ही रुका और आराम किया।)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Describe 3 things you completed yesterday using regular past verbs (e.g., worked, cleaned, cooked, called, watched).
- **HI**: कल आपने जो 3 काम पूरे किए, उनके बारे में regular past क्रियाओं का उपयोग करके 3 वाक्य बोलें।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day21_assess_001]` **Why is 'Yesterday I work for 8 hours' incorrect?** ➔ *Because 'yesterday' refers to a completed past time and requires the past simple V2 form 'worked'.* [Skill: Tense Accuracy]
- `[day21_assess_002]` **How do you pronounce '-ed' in 'COOKED' versus 'WAITED'?** ➔ *'Cooked' is pronounced with /t/ as one syllable (/kʊkt/), while 'waited' takes the extra /ɪd/ syllable (/ˈweɪ.tɪd/).* [Skill: Phonetic Differentiation]
- `[day21_assess_003]` **What is the spelling rule for regular verbs ending in consonant + y (like study)?** ➔ *Change the 'y' to 'i' and add '-ed' (study -> studied).* [Skill: Spelling Rules]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `REGULAR_PAST_SIMPLE_ED`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14]

## Day 22: High-Frequency Irregular Past Simple (Went, Came, Saw, Bought & Core Irregulars) {#day-22-day_22}
**शीर्षक**: प्रमुख अनियमित भूतकाल क्रियाएँ (Went, Came, Saw, Bought और मुख्य Irregular Verbs)  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.2` | **Est. Duration**: 30 mins | **Prerequisites**: Day 21 (Regular Past Simple -ed)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Identify and master the top 15 most frequent irregular past simple verbs (go->went, see->saw, buy->bought, eat->ate, come->came, etc.).
  - **HI**: दैनिक जीवन में सबसे ज़्यादा इस्तेमाल होने वाली 15 Irregular Verbs (Went, Came, Saw, Bought, Ate आदि) पर महारत हासिल करना।
- **EN**: Understand internal vowel shift patterns in irregular verbs (take->took, give->gave, drink->drank, write->wrote).
  - **HI**: अनियमित क्रियाओं में स्वर परिवर्तन (Vowel Shift) के पैटर्न्स को समझना।
- **EN**: Construct accurate affirmative past narratives describing outings, shopping, meals, and social encounters.
  - **HI**: खरीदारी, भोजन, यात्रा और मुलाक़ात से जुड़ी घटनाओं के सटीक भूतकाल वाक्य बनाना।
- **EN**: Avoid the common error of adding '-ed' to irregular verbs (e.g. saying '*goed*' or '*buyed*').
  - **HI**: Irregular क्रियाओं में गलती से '-ed' जोड़ने (जैसे '*goed*' या '*buyed*') से बचना।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
अंग्रेज़ी में कई महत्वपूर्ण और आम क्रियाएँ ऐसी होती हैं जिनमें भूतकाल बनाने के लिए '-ed' नहीं जुड़ता, बल्कि उनका पूरा शब्द या बीच का स्वर बदल जाता है। इन्हें 'Irregular Verbs' (अनियमित क्रियाएँ) कहते हैं। जैसे: Go का Past रूप 'Went' होता है ('goed' कभी नहीं), Buy का 'Bought', See का 'Saw', और Eat का 'Ate'। नियमित क्रियाओं की तरह ही, Irregular Verbs का यह दूसरा रूप (V2) भी सभी कर्ताओं (I, You, He, She, We, They) के साथ बिल्कुल एक जैसा रहता है।

**English Explanation**:
Unlike regular verbs that add '-ed', irregular verbs undergo internal vowel mutations or completely transform their base form in the past simple (V2). For example: go -> went, see -> saw, buy -> bought, have -> had, take -> took, give -> gave, eat -> ate, write -> wrote. While irregular verbs must be memorized, they follow recognizable sound clusters (e.g. buy/bring/think -> bought/brought/thought; sing/drink/swim -> sang/drank/swam). In affirmative sentences, the V2 form is identical across all subjects.

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: Unique Past Forms (No '-ed' Suffix)** [Morphological Standard]
  Irregular verbs never take '-ed'. Saying '*goed*', '*buyed*', '*eated*', or '*seed*' is ungrammatical.
- **Rule 2: Uniform Subject Invariance** [Syntax Standard]
  The irregular past form is unchanged regardless of the subject: I went, she went, we went, they went.
- **Rule 3: Ought/Aught Vowel Pattern (/ɔːt/)** [Phonological Cluster]
  A major cluster of high-frequency verbs changes to '-ought' or '-aught': buy -> bought, bring -> brought, catch -> caught, teach -> taught, think -> thought.
- **Rule 4: Vowel Shift Patterns (A -> E -> O)** [Phonological Pattern]
  Common shifts include: /iː/ to /e/ (meet -> met, read -> read /red/), /ɪ/ to /æ/ (drink -> drank, sit -> sat), /aɪ/ to /oʊ/ (write -> wrote, drive -> drove).

#### Master Formula Table:
```json
[
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Go / Come / Leave (Movement)",
    "formula": "Subject + went / came / left + [Destination/Time]",
    "exampleEn": "We went to the market yesterday.",
    "meaningHi": "हम कल बाज़ार गए।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Buy / Bring / Take (Possession/Exchange)",
    "formula": "Subject + bought / brought / took + [Object]",
    "exampleEn": "She bought a new dress.",
    "meaningHi": "उसने एक नई ड्रेस खरीदी।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Eat / Drink / Have (Consumption)",
    "formula": "Subject + ate / drank / had + [Food/Drink]",
    "exampleEn": "I ate a sandwich for lunch.",
    "meaningHi": "मैंने दोपहर के खाने में सैंडविच खाया।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "See / Meet / Hear (Perception/Social)",
    "formula": "Subject + saw / met / heard + [Person/Event]",
    "exampleEn": "Rohan met his old teacher.",
    "meaningHi": "रोहन अपने पुराने शिक्षक से मिला।"
  }
]
```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: Over-generalizing '-ed' (*buyed, *goed) and double past (*did went)
- **Hindi Pattern**: हिन्दी में 'जाना' का 'गया' होता है। अंग्रेज़ी में नए शिक्षार्थी नियम लागू करके 'go' को 'goed' या 'buy' को 'buyed' बना देते हैं।
- **Potential Interference**: `Saying '*I goed to market and buyed apples*' instead of '*I went to the market and bought apples*'.`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: Irregular verbs are historical strong verbs in English and do not conform to the weak '-ed' pattern.
- **Natural English Solution**: **Memorize high-frequency pairs: go -> went, buy -> bought, see -> saw, give -> gave, eat -> ate.**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day22_vocab_001` | **Went** | Verb (V2) | `/went/` | गया / गए / गई | I went to the supermarket yesterday. <br> *मैं कल सुपरमार्केट गया।* |
| `day22_vocab_002` | **Came** | Verb (V2) | `/keɪm/` | आया / आए / आई | My uncle came to our house last night. <br> *मेरे चाचा कल रात हमारे घर आए।* |
| `day22_vocab_003` | **Saw** | Verb (V2) | `/sɔː/` | देखा | We saw an eagle in the park. <br> *हमने पार्क में एक चील देखी।* |
| `day22_vocab_004` | **Bought** | Verb (V2) | `/bɔːt/` | खरीदा | She bought fresh vegetables from the vendor. <br> *उसने विक्रेता से ताज़ी सब्ज़ियाँ खरीदीं।* |
| `day22_vocab_005` | **Took** | Verb (V2) | `/tʊk/` | लिया / ले गया / लगा (समय) | It took thirty minutes to reach the station. <br> *स्टेशन पहुँचने में तीस मिनट लगे।* |
| `day22_vocab_006` | **Gave** | Verb (V2) | `/ɡeɪv/` | दिया | He gave me a valuable book as a gift. <br> *उसने मुझे उपहार के रूप में एक मूल्यवान पुस्तक दी।* |
| `day22_vocab_007` | **Ate** | Verb (V2) | `/eɪt/` | खाया | We ate South Indian food for breakfast. <br> *हमने नाश्ते में दक्षिण भारतीय खाना खाया।* |
| `day22_vocab_008` | **Drank** | Verb (V2) | `/dræŋk/` | पिया | She drank two cups of green tea. <br> *उसने दो कप ग्रीन टी पी।* |
| `day22_vocab_009` | **Met** | Verb (V2) | `/met/` | मिला / मुलाक़ात की | I met an old school friend yesterday. <br> *मैं कल एक पुराने स्कूल के दोस्त से मिला।* |
| `day22_vocab_010` | **Wrote** | Verb (V2) | `/roʊt/` | लिखा | Ravi wrote an important email to his manager. <br> *रवि ने अपने मैनेजर को एक ज़रूरी ईमेल लिखा।* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day22_phrase_001` [Conversational]: **"I went shopping."** — *मैं खरीदारी करने गया था।*
- `day22_phrase_002` [Conversational]: **"We saw a great movie."** — *हमने एक शानदार फ़िल्म देखी।*
- `day22_phrase_003` [Conversational]: **"He gave me his keys."** — *उसने मुझे अपनी चाबियाँ दीं।*
- `day22_phrase_004` [Conversational]: **"I ate lunch early."** — *मैंने दोपहर का खाना जल्दी खा लिया।*
- `day22_phrase_005` [Conversational]: **"We met at the metro station."** — *हम मेट्रो स्टेशन पर मिले।*
- `day22_phrase_006` [Conversational]: **"It took a long time."** — *इसमें काफ़ी समय लगा।*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day22_example_001`: **I went to Delhi last month.**  
  *मैं पिछले महीने दिल्ली गया था।*
- `day22_example_002`: **She saw a doctor yesterday afternoon.**  
  *उसने कल दोपहर डॉक्टर से मुलाक़ात की (दिखाया)।*
- `day22_example_003`: **We bought a new refrigerator last week.**  
  *हमने पिछले हफ़्ते एक नया फ्रिज खरीदा।*
- `day22_example_004`: **He gave his friend some good advice.**  
  *उसने अपने दोस्त को कुछ अच्छी सलाह दी।*
- `day22_example_005`: **I ate dinner at eight o'clock yesterday.**  
  *मैंने कल आठ बजे रात का खाना खाया।*
- `day22_example_006`: **They came to our wedding ceremony.**  
  *वे हमारे विवाह समारोह में आए थे।*
- `day22_example_007`: **She took an umbrella because it was cloudy.**  
  *उसने छाता ले लिया क्योंकि बादल छाए हुए थे।*
- `day22_example_008`: **I drank a glass of warm milk before bed.**  
  *मैंने सोने से पहले एक गिलास गर्म दूध पिया।*
- `day22_example_009`: **Sunil met his business partner in Mumbai.**  
  *सुनील मुंबई में अपने बिजनेस पार्टनर से मिला।*
- `day22_example_010`: **She wrote a letter to the principal.**  
  *उसने प्रधानाचार्य को एक पत्र लिखा।*
- `day22_example_011`: **We had a wonderful dinner together.**  
  *हमने साथ में शानदार डिनर किया।*
- `day22_example_012`: **He left his office at six sharp.**  
  *उसने ठीक छह बजे अपना दफ़्तर छोड़ा।*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Vowel /ɔː/ in 'saw' and 'bought', Open front /æ/ in 'drank', 'ran', 'sang', Close mid /e/ in 'met', 'left', 'said'
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: 'Bought' has a silent 'gh' and rhymes with 'caught' /bɔːt/. Do not pronounce the 'g' or 'h'. 'Read' in past tense is pronounced /red/ (like the color red).
#### Audio Drills:
- `day22_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "I bought a book and brought it home."
- `day22_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "We went to the cafe and ate lunch."
- `day22_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "She saw her friends and drank some tea."
- `day22_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "He took the train and came on time."

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (35 Items)
#### 1. `[day22_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `IRREGULAR_PAST_AFFIRMATIVE`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct sentence to say 'हम कल बाज़ार गए':
**Options**: `We goed to the market yesterday.` | `We went to the market yesterday.` | `We were go to the market yesterday.` | `We wented to the market yesterday.`
- **Correct Answer**: `We went to the market yesterday.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Go' is irregular; its past simple form is 'went'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Go' एक अनियमित क्रिया है, इसका भूतकाल 'went' होता है।

#### 2. `[day22_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `IRREGULAR_PAST_AFFIRMATIVE`)
**Prompt**: What is the past simple of 'Buy'?
**Options**: `Buyed` | `Bought` | `Brought` | `Buying`
- **Correct Answer**: `Bought`
- **Explanation (EN)**: The past tense of 'buy' is 'bought' (pronounced /bɔːt/).
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Buy' का Past Simple रूप 'bought' होता है।

#### 3. `[day22_ex_003]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `IRREGULAR_PAST_AFFIRMATIVE`)
**Prompt**: Complete the sentence: 'She ______ (see) an accident on the highway yesterday.'
- **Correct Answer**: `saw`
- **Explanation (EN)**: The past simple form of 'see' is 'saw'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'See' का Past Simple रूप 'saw' है।

#### 4. `[day22_ex_004]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `IRREGULAR_PAST_AFFIRMATIVE`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank: 'Rohan ______ (give) me a pen during the exam.'
- **Correct Answer**: `gave`
- **Explanation (EN)**: The past simple of 'give' is 'gave'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Give' का भूतकाल रूप 'gave' होता है।

#### 5. `[day22_ex_005]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `IRREGULAR_PAST_AFFIRMATIVE`)
**Prompt**: Which past form is spelled and used CORRECTLY?
**Options**: `Ate (from eat)` | `Eated (from eat)` | `Seed (from see)` | `Takened (from take)`
- **Correct Answer**: `Ate (from eat)`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Eat' changes to 'ate' in past simple.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Eat' का Past रूप 'ate' होता है।

#### 6. `[day22_ex_006]` [WORD_REORDER] (medium | `SENTENCE_BUILDING`)
**Prompt**: Put the words in correct order: [yesterday / a new phone / bought / Manish]
**Options**: `Manish bought a new phone yesterday.` | `Manish yesterday a new phone bought.` | `Bought Manish a new phone yesterday.` | `A new phone Manish bought yesterday.`
- **Correct Answer**: `Manish bought a new phone yesterday.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Subject (Manish) + V2 (bought) + Object (a new phone) + Time (yesterday).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: Manish bought a new phone yesterday.

#### 7. `[day22_ex_007]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_DETECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'I drink three glasses of water last night.'
- **Correct Answer**: `I drank three glasses of water last night.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Past tense of 'drink' is 'drank' to match 'last night'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Last night' के कारण 'drink' का भूतकाल रूप 'drank' होगा।

#### 8. `[day22_ex_008]` [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (easy | `TRANSLATION_HI_EN`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'वह कल रात हमारे घर आया।'
**Options**: `He came to our house last night.` | `He comed to our house last night.` | `He was come to our house last night.` | `He comes to our house last night.`
- **Correct Answer**: `He came to our house last night.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'वह आया' = 'He came', 'कल रात' = 'last night'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अनुवाद: 'He came to our house last night.'

#### 9. `[day22_ex_009]` [ENGLISH_TO_HINDI] (easy | `TRANSLATION_EN_HI`)
**Prompt**: What is the Hindi meaning of: 'She took a taxi to the airport'?
**Options**: `वह हवाई अड्डे के लिए टैक्सी से गई (टैक्सी ली)।` | `वह हवाई अड्डे पर टैक्सी चला रही थी।` | `उसने टैक्सी नहीं ली।` | `वह हवाई अड्डे से पैदल आई।`
- **Correct Answer**: `वह हवाई अड्डे के लिए टैक्सी से गई (टैक्सी ली)।`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Took a taxi' means 'टैक्सी ली / टैक्सी से गई'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अर्थ: 'वह हवाई अड्डे के लिए टैक्सी से गई (टैक्सी ली)।'

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: Catching up with a friend over coffee to discuss weekend shopping and dining experiences.

#### Dialogue (`day22_dialogue_001`):
- **Vikram**: "Hey Ankit, what did you do on Saturday?"  
  *(अरे अंकित, तुमने शनिवार को क्या किया?)*
- **Ankit**: "I went to the mall with my brother. We bought some new shirts and shoes."  
  *(मैं अपने भाई के साथ मॉल गया था। हमने कुछ नई शर्ट्स और जूते खरीदे।)*
- **Vikram**: "Nice! Did you eat lunch there?"  
  *(बहुत बढ़िया! क्या तुमने वहाँ दोपहर का खाना खाया?)*
- **Ankit**: "Yes, we ate pizza at an Italian restaurant and drank fresh mango juice. Then we saw a movie."  
  *(हाँ, हमने एक इटैलियन रेस्टोरेंट में पिज़्ज़ा खाया और ताज़ा आम का जूस पिया। फिर हमने एक फ़िल्म देखी।)*
- **Vikram**: "Sounds like you had a great day! I stayed home and wrote my weekly report."  
  *(लगता है तुम्हारा दिन बहुत अच्छा बीता! मैं घर पर रुका और अपनी साप्ताहिक रिपोर्ट लिखी।)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Tell a short 3-sentence story about your last shopping trip or outing using at least 3 irregular verbs (went, bought, ate, saw, met).
- **HI**: कम से कम 3 irregular क्रियाओं (went, bought, ate, saw, met) का उपयोग करके अपनी पिछली खरीदारी या यात्रा के बारे में 3 वाक्यों की कहानी बोलें।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day22_assess_001]` **Why is 'I goed to the market and buyed fruit' incorrect in English?** ➔ *Because 'go' and 'buy' are irregular verbs; their past simple forms are 'went' and 'bought', never with '-ed'.* [Skill: Irregular Verb Accuracy]
- `[day22_assess_002]` **How does the pronunciation of 'READ' change between present tense and past tense?** ➔ *Present tense 'read' is pronounced /riːd/ (rhymes with seed), while past tense 'read' is pronounced /red/ (rhymes with red).* [Skill: Phonetic Differentiation]
- `[day22_assess_003]` **Give the past simple forms of: Catch, Bring, and Teach.** ➔ *Caught (/kɔːt/), Brought (/brɔːt/), and Taught (/tɔːt/).* [Skill: Ought/Aught Morphological Pattern]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `IRREGULAR_PAST_SIMPLE_V2`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14]

## Day 23: Past Simple Negative (Didn't + Base Verb V1 & Eliminating Double Past Traps) {#day-23-day_23}
**शीर्षक**: भूतकाल के नकारात्मक वाक्य (Didn't + क्रिया का मूल रूप V1 और Double Past की गलतियों से बचाव)  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.2` | **Est. Duration**: 30 mins | **Prerequisites**: Day 21–22 (Past Simple Regular & Irregular)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Form accurate negative sentences in the past simple using 'did not / didn't + Base Verb (V1)' across all subjects.
  - **HI**: सभी कर्ताओं के साथ 'didn't + क्रिया का मूल रूप (V1)' का प्रयोग करके भूतकाल के नकारात्मक वाक्य बनाना।
- **EN**: Master the non-negotiable rule: the main verb MUST revert to its base form (V1) after 'didn't'.
  - **HI**: यह नियम पक्का करना कि 'didn't' आने पर मुख्य क्रिया हमेशा अपने मूल रूप (V1) में लौट आती है।
- **EN**: Eliminate the pervasive double-past error (saying '*didn't went*', '*didn't called*', or '*didn't bought*').
  - **HI**: अक्सर होने वाली 'Double Past' की गलती (जैसे '*didn't went*', '*didn't saw*') को पूरी तरह समाप्त करना।
- **EN**: Express negative past possession correctly using 'didn't have' (eliminating '*didn't had*').
  - **HI**: भूतकाल में किसी चीज़ के न होने पर 'didn't have' का सही प्रयोग करना ('*didn't had*' से बचना)।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
जब हम भूतकाल में कोई काम 'नहीं किया' की बात करते हैं (जैसे: 'मैं कल नहीं गया', 'उसने मुझे फ़ोन नहीं किया', 'हमने खाना नहीं खाया'), तो अंग्रेज़ी में सहायक क्रिया 'Did' के साथ 'Not' जोड़कर 'Didn't' का प्रयोग करते हैं। सबसे महत्वपूर्ण नियम: चूँकि 'Did' पहले से ही भूतकाल (Do का V2) दर्शा रहा है, इसलिए इसके बाद आने वाली मुख्य क्रिया हमेशा अपने मूल रूप (Base Form V1) में आती है! कभी भी 'didn't' के बाद V2 न लगाएँ—'*I didn't went*' बोलना बिल्कुल गलत है, सही वाक्य होगा: 'I didn't go'!

**English Explanation**:
To negate an action in the Past Simple, we use the auxiliary operator DID + NOT (contracted as 'didn't') followed by the BASE FORM of the verb (V1). Because 'did' already carries the past tense marker, the main verb must strictly revert to its bare infinitive form. This rule applies uniformly to all subjects (I, you, he, she, it, we, they) and to both regular and irregular verbs. Saying '*didn't went*' or '*didn't worked*' is known as the 'Double Past Error' and is ungrammatical.

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: Uniform Auxiliary 'Didn't' for All Subjects** [Universal Syntax Rule]
  The negative auxiliary in the past simple is universally 'didn't' (did not) for all pronouns: I didn't, you didn't, he didn't, we didn't, they didn't.
- **Rule 2: The Absolute Base Verb Principle (Didn't + V1)** [Core Grammatical Invariant]
  After 'didn't', the main lexical verb MUST return to V1 base form. Correct: 'She didn't call.' Incorrect: '*She didn't called.*' Correct: 'I didn't see.' Incorrect: '*I didn't saw.*'
- **Rule 3: Negative Past Possession (Didn't Have)** [Standard Usage]
  To express absence of possession in the past, use 'didn't have'. Correct: 'We didn't have enough money.' Never say: '*We didn't had money.*'
- **Rule 4: BE Verb Past Negation (Wasn't / Weren't) Contrast** [Structural Discrimination]
  Do not use 'didn't' with states of being. Say 'I wasn't ready' (state), NOT '*I didn't ready*'. Use 'didn't' only with dynamic action verbs.

#### Master Formula Table:
```json
[
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Regular Action Verbs",
    "formula": "Subject + didn't + Base Verb (V1) + [Object] + [Time]",
    "exampleEn": "He didn't clean his room yesterday.",
    "meaningHi": "उसने कल अपना कमरा साफ़ नहीं किया।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Irregular Action Verbs",
    "formula": "Subject + didn't + Base Verb (V1) + [Object]",
    "exampleEn": "I didn't go to the market.",
    "meaningHi": "मैं बाज़ार नहीं गया।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Past Possession Negation",
    "formula": "Subject + didn't have + [Noun Object]",
    "exampleEn": "She didn't have her ID card.",
    "meaningHi": "उसके पास उसका पहचान पत्र नहीं था।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Past State (BE Verb Contrast)",
    "formula": "Subject + wasn't / weren't + [Adjective/Noun]",
    "exampleEn": "They weren't at home last night.",
    "meaningHi": "वे कल रात घर पर नहीं थे।"
  }
]
```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: The Double Past Error ('didn't went', 'didn't told') and using 'not' directly
- **Hindi Pattern**: हिन्दी में 'मैं नहीं गया' में 'गया' (भूतकाल) रहता है, इसलिए भारतीय शिक्षार्थी अंग्रेज़ी में 'didn't' के बाद भी भूतकाल क्रिया 'went' या 'called' बोल देते हैं।
- **Potential Interference**: `Saying '*I didn't went there*' or '*She didn't informed me*'.`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: English grammar allows only ONE past tense marker per simple predicate. 'Did' already carries the past inflection, so the main verb must be stripped to base V1.
- **Natural English Solution**: **Always say 'I didn't go' and 'She didn't inform me'.**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day23_vocab_001` | **Hear** | Verb | `/hɪər/` | सुनना | I didn't hear the doorbell ring. <br> *मैंने दरवाज़े की घंटी बजते नहीं सुनी।* |
| `day23_vocab_002` | **Find** | Verb | `/faɪnd/` | पाना / मिलना | He didn't find his lost car keys. <br> *उसे अपनी खोई हुई कार की चाबियाँ नहीं मिलीं।* |
| `day23_vocab_003` | **Receive** | Verb | `/rɪˈsiːv/` | प्राप्त करना / मिलना | We didn't receive your email yesterday. <br> *हमें कल आपका ईमेल प्राप्त नहीं हुआ।* |
| `day23_vocab_004` | **Sleep** | Verb | `/sliːp/` | सोना | She didn't sleep well last night. <br> *वह कल रात ठीक से नहीं सोई।* |
| `day23_vocab_005` | **Attend** | Verb | `/əˈtend/` | उपस्थित होना / भाग लेना | Rohan didn't attend the meeting on Monday. <br> *रोहन सोमवार को मीटिंग में शामिल नहीं हुआ।* |
| `day23_vocab_006` | **Spend** | Verb | `/spend/` | खर्च करना / बिताना | They didn't spend much money on shopping. <br> *उन्होंने खरीदारी पर ज़्यादा पैसे खर्च नहीं किए।* |
| `day23_vocab_007` | **Leave** | Verb | `/liːv/` | निकलना / छोड़ना | The train didn't leave on schedule. <br> *ट्रेन तय समय पर नहीं निकली।* |
| `day23_vocab_008` | **Answer** | Verb | `/ˈɑːn.sər/` | उत्तर देना / फ़ोन उठाना | She didn't answer my phone call. <br> *उसने मेरा फ़ोन नहीं उठाया।* |
| `day23_vocab_009` | **Bring** | Verb | `/brɪŋ/` | लाना | I didn't bring my umbrella today. <br> *मैं आज अपना छाता नहीं लाया।* |
| `day23_vocab_010` | **Pay** | Verb | `/peɪ/` | भुगतान करना / चुकाना | He didn't pay the bill at the restaurant. <br> *उसने रेस्टोरेंट में बिल का भुगतान नहीं किया।* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day23_phrase_001` [Conversational]: **"I didn't know that."** — *मुझे वह नहीं पता था।*
- `day23_phrase_002` [Conversational]: **"She didn't answer my call."** — *उसने मेरा फ़ोन नहीं उठाया।*
- `day23_phrase_003` [Conversational]: **"We didn't have enough time."** — *हमारे पास पर्याप्त समय नहीं था।*
- `day23_phrase_004` [Conversational]: **"He didn't come to work."** — *वह काम पर नहीं आया।*
- `day23_phrase_005` [Conversational]: **"I didn't receive your message."** — *मुझे आपका संदेश नहीं मिला।*
- `day23_phrase_006` [Conversational]: **"They didn't buy anything."** — *उन्होंने कुछ भी नहीं खरीदा।*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day23_example_001`: **I didn't go to the gym yesterday.**  
  *मैं कल जिम नहीं गया।*
- `day23_example_002`: **She didn't call me back last night.**  
  *उसने कल रात मुझे वापस फ़ोन नहीं किया।*
- `day23_example_003`: **We didn't see any wild animals in the forest.**  
  *हमने जंगल में कोई जंगली जानवर नहीं देखा।*
- `day23_example_004`: **Rahul didn't eat breakfast this morning.**  
  *राहुल ने आज सुबह नाश्ता नहीं किया।*
- `day23_example_005`: **They didn't buy the expensive tickets.**  
  *उन्होंने महंगे टिकट नहीं खरीदे।*
- `day23_example_006`: **I didn't have my wallet with me.**  
  *मेरे पास मेरा बटुआ नहीं था।*
- `day23_example_007`: **The bus didn't stop at our gate.**  
  *बस हमारे गेट पर नहीं रुकी।*
- `day23_example_008`: **She didn't understand the teacher's instructions.**  
  *उसे शिक्षक के निर्देश समझ नहीं आए।*
- `day23_example_009`: **My parents didn't watch TV last evening.**  
  *मेरे माता-पिता ने कल शाम टीवी नहीं देखा।*
- `day23_example_010`: **He didn't write the final exam.**  
  *उसने अंतिम परीक्षा नहीं लिखी।*
- `day23_example_011`: **We didn't take any photographs at the museum.**  
  *हमने संग्रहालय में कोई तस्वीरें नहीं लीं।*
- `day23_example_012`: **I didn't feel well yesterday afternoon.**  
  *कल दोपहर मेरी तबीयत ठीक नहीं लग रही थी।*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Contracted /ˈdɪd.ənt/ with reduced schwa or glottal stop, Clean transition between 'didn't' and following base verb
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: Do not separate 'did not' heavily in natural conversation. 'Didn't' is pronounced as two quick syllables /ˈdɪd.ənt/ or /dɪdnt/.
#### Audio Drills:
- `day23_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "I didn't go, and she didn't call."
- `day23_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "We didn't have enough time yesterday."
- `day23_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "He didn't see the email this morning."
- `day23_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "They didn't buy anything from that shop."

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (35 Items)
#### 1. `[day23_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PAST_SIMPLE_NEGATION`)
**Prompt**: Choose the grammatically CORRECT sentence for 'मैं कल दफ़्तर नहीं गया':
**Options**: `I didn't went to the office yesterday.` | `I didn't go to the office yesterday.` | `I not went to the office yesterday.` | `I didn't goes to the office yesterday.`
- **Correct Answer**: `I didn't go to the office yesterday.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: After 'didn't', the verb must be in its base form 'go' (V1).
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Didn't' के बाद हमेशा क्रिया का मूल रूप 'go' आता है, 'went' कभी नहीं।

#### 2. `[day23_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PAST_SIMPLE_NEGATION`)
**Prompt**: Which sentence correctly eliminates the Double Past Error?
**Options**: `She didn't called me.` | `She didn't call me.` | `She didn't calls me.` | `She not call me.`
- **Correct Answer**: `She didn't call me.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Didn't call' is correct because 'did' already signals past tense.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Didn't' के बाद V1 'call' सही है।

#### 3. `[day23_ex_003]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `PAST_SIMPLE_NEGATION`)
**Prompt**: Complete the sentence with the base verb of (eat): 'Rohan didn't ______ anything all morning.'
- **Correct Answer**: `eat`
- **Explanation (EN)**: The base form after 'didn't' is 'eat', not 'ate'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Didn't' के बाद 'eat' आएगा, 'ate' नहीं।

#### 4. `[day23_ex_004]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (medium | `PAST_POSSESSION_NEGATION`)
**Prompt**: How do you express negative past possession: 'हमारे पास पैसे नहीं थे'?
**Options**: `We didn't had money.` | `We didn't have money.` | `We not had money.` | `We hadn't no money.`
- **Correct Answer**: `We didn't have money.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Negative past possession uses 'didn't have' (base form 'have').
- **Explanation (HI)**: भूतकाल के नकारात्मक स्वामित्व में 'didn't have' का प्रयोग होता है।

#### 5. `[day23_ex_005]` [WORD_REORDER] (medium | `SENTENCE_BUILDING`)
**Prompt**: Put the words in correct order: [yesterday / receive / we / your message / didn't]
**Options**: `We didn't receive your message yesterday.` | `We didn't your message receive yesterday.` | `Yesterday didn't we receive your message.` | `We received not your message yesterday.`
- **Correct Answer**: `We didn't receive your message yesterday.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Subject (We) + didn't + V1 (receive) + object (your message) + time (yesterday).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: We didn't receive your message yesterday.

#### 6. `[day23_ex_006]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_DETECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the mistake: 'I didn't saw the traffic signal.'
- **Correct Answer**: `I didn't see the traffic signal.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Saw' must revert to the base form 'see' after 'didn't'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Didn't' के बाद 'saw' बदलकर मूल रूप 'see' होगा।

#### 7. `[day23_ex_007]` [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (easy | `TRANSLATION_HI_EN`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'उसने कल रात खाना नहीं पकाया।'
**Options**: `She didn't cook food last night.` | `She didn't cooked food last night.` | `She was not cook food last night.` | `She not cook food last night.`
- **Correct Answer**: `She didn't cook food last night.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Didn't cook' with base verb V1.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अनुवाद: 'She didn't cook food last night.'

#### 8. `[day23_ex_008]` [ENGLISH_TO_HINDI] (easy | `TRANSLATION_EN_HI`)

- **Correct Answer**: `उन्हें सवाल समझ नहीं आया।`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Didn't understand' means 'समझ नहीं आया'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अर्थ: 'उन्हें सवाल समझ नहीं आया।'

- **Correct Answer**: `buy`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Base verb 'buy' is used after 'didn't'.

- **Correct Answer**: `He didn't brought his laptop.`

- **Correct Answer**: `She didn't answer my phone last night.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Subject + didn't + V1 + object + time.

- **Correct Answer**: `Rohan didn't take the exam yesterday.`

- **Explanation (HI)**: सही वाक्य: 'Rohan didn't take the exam yesterday.'

**Prompt**: Fill in the blank: 'They didn't ______ (leave) the party early.'
- **Correct Answer**: `leave`

**Prompt**: Translate: 'मुझे आपका संदेश नहीं मिला।'
**Options**: `I didn't receive your message.` | `I didn't received your message.` | `I was not receive your message.` | `I not received your message.`
- **Correct Answer**: `I didn't receive your message.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Correct translation: 'I didn't receive your message.'
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अनुवाद: 'I didn't receive your message.'

#### 18. `[day23_ex_018]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PAST_POSSESSION_NEGATION`)
**Prompt**: Which sentence correctly expresses absence of time yesterday?
**Options**: `I didn't have time yesterday.` | `I didn't had time yesterday.` | `I not have time yesterday.` | `I had no not time yesterday.`
- **Correct Answer**: `I didn't have time yesterday.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Didn't have' uses the base form 'have'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Didn't have' बिल्कुल सही है।

#### 19. `[day23_ex_019]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_DETECTION`)
**Prompt**: Fix the mistake: 'My brother didn't came home for dinner.'
- **Correct Answer**: `My brother didn't come home for dinner.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Came' must be replaced by 'come' after 'didn't'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Didn't' के बाद 'came' बदलकर 'come' होगा।

#### 20. `[day23_ex_020]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `PAST_SIMPLE_NEGATION`)
**Prompt**: Complete the sentence: 'The doctor didn't ______ (come) to the clinic yesterday.'
- **Correct Answer**: `come`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Base form 'come'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Didn't' के बाद 'come' आएगा।

#### 21. `[day23_ex_021]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PAST_SIMPLE_NEGATION`)
**Prompt**: Select the sentence with ACCURATE past negation:
**Options**: `We didn't spend any money at the fair.` | `We didn't spent any money at the fair.` | `We not spent any money at the fair.` | `We wasn't spend any money at the fair.`
- **Correct Answer**: `We didn't spend any money at the fair.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Didn't spend' uses base verb 'spend'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Didn't spend' पूर्णतः शुद्ध वाक्य है।

#### 22. `[day23_ex_022]` [WORD_REORDER] (easy | `SENTENCE_BUILDING`)
**Prompt**: Arrange in order: [the loud noise / didn't / hear / I]
**Options**: `I didn't hear the loud noise.` | `I hear didn't the loud noise.` | `The loud noise didn't I hear.` | `Didn't I hear the loud noise.`
- **Correct Answer**: `I didn't hear the loud noise.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Subject (I) + didn't + V1 (hear) + object (the loud noise).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: I didn't hear the loud noise.

#### 23. `[day23_ex_023]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_DETECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and fix the error: 'She didn't paid the electricity bill.'
- **Correct Answer**: `She didn't pay the electricity bill.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Paid' must revert to 'pay' after 'didn't'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Didn't' के बाद 'paid' बदलकर 'pay' होगा।

#### 24. `[day23_ex_024]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `PAST_SIMPLE_NEGATION`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank: 'I ______ (not / sleep) well because of the storm.'
- **Correct Answer**: `didn't sleep`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `did not sleep`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Past simple negative of 'sleep' is 'didn't sleep'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Sleep' का Past Simple Negative 'didn't sleep' है।

#### 25. `[day23_ex_025]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PAST_SIMPLE_NEGATION`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct sentence to say 'वे बैठक में शामिल नहीं हुए':
**Options**: `They didn't attend the meeting.` | `They didn't attended the meeting.` | `They not attend the meeting.` | `They wasn't attend the meeting.`
- **Correct Answer**: `They didn't attend the meeting.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Didn't attend' with base verb V1.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही वाक्य: 'They didn't attend the meeting.'

#### 26. `[day23_ex_026]` [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (easy | `TRANSLATION_HI_EN`)
**Prompt**: Translate: 'उसने मुझे सच नहीं बताया।'
**Options**: `He didn't tell me the truth.` | `He didn't told me the truth.` | `He not tell me the truth.` | `He was not tell me the truth.`
- **Correct Answer**: `He didn't tell me the truth.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Didn't tell' with base verb V1.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अनुवाद: 'He didn't tell me the truth.'

#### 27. `[day23_ex_027]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_DETECTION`)
**Prompt**: Fix the error: 'The bus driver didn't stopped at the red light.'
- **Correct Answer**: `The bus driver didn't stop at the red light.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Stopped' must revert to 'stop' after 'didn't'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Didn't' के बाद 'stopped' बदलकर 'stop' होगा।

#### 28. `[day23_ex_028]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `PAST_SIMPLE_NEGATION`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank: 'We didn't ______ (find) any parking space near the mall.'
- **Correct Answer**: `find`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Base verb 'find'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Didn't' के बाद 'find' आएगा।

#### 29. `[day23_ex_029]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PAST_SIMPLE_NEGATION`)
**Prompt**: Which pair correctly contrasts affirmative and negative past?
**Options**: `I went -> I didn't go` | `I went -> I didn't went` | `I ate -> I didn't ate` | `I bought -> I didn't bought`
- **Correct Answer**: `I went -> I didn't go`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Affirmative 'went' inverts to negative 'didn't go'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'I went' का नकारात्मक 'I didn't go' होता है।

#### 30. `[day23_ex_030]` [WORD_REORDER] (medium | `SENTENCE_BUILDING`)
**Prompt**: Reorder: [yesterday / didn't / he / his medicine / take]
**Options**: `He didn't take his medicine yesterday.` | `He didn't his medicine take yesterday.` | `Yesterday take didn't he his medicine.` | `He took not his medicine yesterday.`
- **Correct Answer**: `He didn't take his medicine yesterday.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Subject + didn't + V1 + Object + Time.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: He didn't take his medicine yesterday.

#### 31. `[day23_ex_031]` [DIALOGUE_COMPLETION] (easy | `CONVERSATIONAL_USE`)
**Prompt**: Complete the dialogue: Manager: 'Why isn't the report submitted?' Employee: 'I am sorry, sir. My computer broke down, so I ______ (not / finish) it yesterday.'
- **Correct Answer**: `didn't finish`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `did not finish`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use past negative 'didn't finish'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: कल के काम के लिए 'didn't finish' आएगा।

#### 32. `[day23_ex_032]` [SPOKEN_RESPONSE] (easy | `SPEAKING`)
**Prompt**: Speak aloud: State two things you did NOT do yesterday using 'Yesterday, I didn't go...' or 'Yesterday, I didn't watch...'.
- **Correct Answer**: `Yesterday, I didn't [V1 verb] and I didn't [V1 verb].`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'didn't + V1' to describe actions you did not perform yesterday.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Yesterday, I didn't [V1 verb]...' बोलकर दो वाक्य बताएं।

#### 33. `[day23_ex_033]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: What is the negative form of 'She bought a car'?
- **Correct Answer**: `She didn't buy a car.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `She did not buy a car.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Bought' changes to 'didn't buy'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'She bought a car' का नकारात्मक 'She didn't buy a car' होता है।

#### 34. `[day23_ex_034]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: What is the negative form of 'We had a meeting'?
- **Correct Answer**: `We didn't have a meeting.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `We did not have a meeting.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Had' becomes 'didn't have'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'We had a meeting' का नकारात्मक 'We didn't have a meeting' होता है।

#### 35. `[day23_ex_035]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: What is the negative form of 'He saw the doctor'?
- **Correct Answer**: `He didn't see the doctor.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `He did not see the doctor.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Saw' becomes 'didn't see'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'He saw the doctor' का नकारात्मक 'He didn't see the doctor' होता है।

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: Two colleagues discussing why a project deliverable was delayed and clarifying misunderstandings.

#### Dialogue (`day23_dialogue_001`):
- **Meera**: "Karan, why didn't you attend the client meeting yesterday afternoon?"  
  *(करण, तुम कल दोपहर क्लाइंट मीटिंग में क्यों नहीं आए?)*
- **Karan**: "I am really sorry, Meera. I didn't receive the calendar invite on my phone."  
  *(मुझे सचमुच खेद है, मीरा। मुझे अपने फ़ोन पर कैलेंडर आमंत्रण नहीं मिला था।)*
- **Meera**: "Oh, I see. Did you call Rahul to ask for the notes?"  
  *(अरे, समझी। क्या तुमने राहुल को फ़ोन करके नोट्स माँगे?)*
- **Karan**: "I called him twice, but he didn't answer his phone. So I didn't know the new project deadlines."  
  *(मैंने उसे दो बार फ़ोन किया, लेकिन उसने फ़ोन नहीं उठाया। इसलिए मुझे प्रोजेक्ट की नई समय-सीमा का पता नहीं चला।)*
- **Meera**: "Don't worry. I have the summary document right here. We didn't finalize everything yet."  
  *(चिंता मत करो। मेरे पास सारांश दस्तावेज़ यहीं है। हमने अभी सब कुछ अंतिम रूप से तय नहीं किया है।)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Describe 3 things that you did NOT do yesterday using 'didn't + V1' (e.g. didn't go, didn't eat, didn't call, didn't watch).
- **HI**: कल आपने जो 3 काम नहीं किए, उनके बारे में 'didn't + V1' का उपयोग करके 3 वाक्य बोलें।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day23_assess_001]` **What is the 'Double Past Error' and why is 'I didn't went' incorrect?** ➔ *The Double Past Error occurs when both the auxiliary 'didn't' and the main verb are in past tense. 'Didn't' already marks the past, so the main verb must remain in its base form 'go'.* [Skill: Syntax Integrity]
- `[day23_assess_002]` **How do you correctly negate past possession: 'He had a laptop'?** ➔ *He didn't have a laptop (using base form 'have', never '*didn't had*').* [Skill: Possession Accuracy]
- `[day23_assess_003]` **What is the difference between 'I wasn't hungry' and 'I didn't eat'?** ➔ *'Wasn't hungry' negates a past state/adjective (BE verb), while 'didn't eat' negates a past dynamic action verb.* [Skill: Grammar Discrimination]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `PAST_SIMPLE_NEGATION_DIDNT`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14]

## Day 24: Past Simple Questions & Short Answers (Did you...? & Yes, I did / No, I didn't) {#day-24-day_24}
**शीर्षक**: भूतकाल के प्रश्न और संक्षिप्त उत्तर (Did you...? और Yes, I did / No, I didn't)  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.2` | **Est. Duration**: 30 mins | **Prerequisites**: Day 21–23 (Past Simple Affirmative & Negative)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Form accurate Yes/No questions in the past simple using auxiliary inversion: 'Did + Subject + Base Verb (V1)...?'.
  - **HI**: सहायक क्रिया 'Did' को कर्ता के आगे रखकर Yes/No प्रश्न बनाना: 'Did + Subject + Base Verb (V1)...?'।
- **EN**: Produce natural short answers ('Yes, I did.' / 'No, I didn't.') without echoing the full verb.
  - **HI**: प्राकृतिक संक्षिप्त उत्तर देना ('Yes, I did.' / 'No, I didn't.')।
- **EN**: Master the base verb rule in questions: ensure the main verb stays in V1 (avoiding '*Did you went?*').
  - **HI**: प्रश्नों में भी क्रिया के मूल रूप (V1) का नियम लागू करना ('*Did you went?*' जैसी गलती से बचना)।
- **EN**: Inquire politely about past completed activities, travel, and meals in daily social conversations.
  - **HI**: दैनिक सामाजिक बातचीत में भोजन, यात्रा और काम के बारे में विनम्रता से भूतकाल के प्रश्न पूछना।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
जब हमें किसी से पूछना हो कि क्या उसने भूतकाल में कोई काम किया (जैसे: 'क्या तुम कल आए थे?', 'क्या तुमने खाना खाया?', 'क्या उसने तुम्हें फ़ोन किया?'), तो अंग्रेज़ी में वाक्य की शुरुआत सहायक क्रिया 'Did' से करते हैं। इसका सूत्र है: 'Did + कर्ता + क्रिया का मूल रूप (V1) + अन्य शब्द?'। ध्यान रहे, यहाँ भी 'Did' भूतकाल दर्शा चुका है, इसलिए मुख्य क्रिया में V2 नहीं लगेगा (जैसे 'Did you see?', '*Did you saw?*' कभी नहीं)। इसके जवाब में पूरा वाक्य दोहराने के बजाय हम संक्षिप्त उत्तर देते हैं: 'Yes, I did.' या 'No, I didn't.'

**English Explanation**:
To form Yes/No questions in the Past Simple, we invert the auxiliary operator DID before the subject: 'Did + Subject + Base Verb (V1) + [Object/Time]?'. Because 'Did' is already in the past tense, the main lexical verb MUST remain in its base form (V1). For short answers, standard English uses the auxiliary without repeating the main verb: 'Yes, I/he/they did.' or 'No, I/he/they didn't.' Rising intonation is used at the end of Yes/No past questions.

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: Auxiliary-Subject Inversion with 'Did'** [Universal Question Syntax]
  All Yes/No past simple questions begin with 'Did' regardless of subject: Did I, Did you, Did he, Did she, Did they.
- **Rule 2: Base Verb (V1) Integrity in Inquiries** [Core Syntax Invariant]
  The main verb following 'Did + Subject' MUST be in base infinitive form (V1). Correct: 'Did you lock the door?' Incorrect: '*Did you locked the door?*'
- **Rule 3: Formulaic Short Answer Structure** [Conversational Pragmatics]
  Affirmative: 'Yes, [subject pronoun] did.' Negative: 'No, [subject pronoun] didn't.' Never say '*Yes, I went*' or '*No, I didn't went*' as a standalone short reply.
- **Rule 4: BE Verb Inversion Contrast (Was/Were)** [Structural Contrast]
  Questions about past states or locations use 'Was/Were + Subject', NOT 'Did'. Say 'Were you happy?' (state), NOT '*Did you be happy?*'.

#### Master Formula Table:
```json
[
  {
    "subjectGroup": "General Action Questions",
    "formula": "Did + [Subject] + Base Verb (V1) + [Object]?",
    "exampleEn": "Did you call the doctor yesterday?",
    "meaningHi": "क्या तुमने कल डॉक्टर को फ़ोन किया था?"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Movement / Travel Questions",
    "formula": "Did + [Subject] + go / arrive / reach + [Place]?",
    "exampleEn": "Did they arrive on time?",
    "meaningHi": "क्या वे समय पर पहुँचे?"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Affirmative Short Answer",
    "formula": "Yes, + [Subject Pronoun] + did.",
    "exampleEn": "Yes, I did. / Yes, she did.",
    "meaningHi": "हाँ, मैंने किया। / हाँ, उसने किया।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Negative Short Answer",
    "formula": "No, + [Subject Pronoun] + didn't.",
    "exampleEn": "No, we didn't. / No, he didn't.",
    "meaningHi": "नहीं, हमने नहीं किया। / नहीं, उसने नहीं किया।"
  }
]
```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: Double Past in Questions ('*Did you went?*') and answering with '*Yes, I did went*'
- **Hindi Pattern**: हिन्दी में 'क्या तुम गए?' में 'गए' (भूतकाल) होता है, इसलिए शिक्षार्थी 'Did' के साथ भी 'went', 'saw' या 'cooked' जोड़ देते हैं।
- **Potential Interference**: `Asking '*Did you ate lunch?*' or '*Did you saw the news?*'.`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: 'Did' is the sole grammatical past marker in the question clause. The lexical verb must be uninflected V1.
- **Natural English Solution**: **Always ask 'Did you eat lunch?' and 'Did you see the news?'.**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day24_vocab_001` | **Enjoy** | Verb | `/ɪnˈdʒɔɪ/` | आनंद लेना / मज़ा आना | Did you enjoy the movie last night? <br> *क्या आपको कल रात फ़िल्म में मज़ा आया?* |
| `day24_vocab_002` | **Lock** | Verb | `/lɒk/` | ताला लगाना | Did you lock the main gate? <br> *क्या तुमने मुख्य दरवाज़े पर ताला लगाया?* |
| `day24_vocab_003` | **Notice** | Verb | `/ˈnəʊ.tɪs/` | ध्यान देना / गौर करना | Did you notice any strange noise? <br> *क्या तुमने कोई अजीब आवाज़ पर गौर किया?* |
| `day24_vocab_004` | **Invite** | Verb | `/ɪnˈvaɪt/` | आमंत्रित करना / न्योता देना | Did they invite you to the party? <br> *क्या उन्होंने तुम्हें पार्टी में आमंत्रित किया था?* |
| `day24_vocab_005` | **Complete** | Verb | `/kəmˈpliːt/` | पूरा करना | Did Priya complete the assignment? <br> *क्या प्रिया ने असाइनमेंट पूरा किया?* |
| `day24_vocab_006` | **Reach** | Verb | `/riːtʃ/` | पहुँचना | Did you reach home safely yesterday? <br> *क्या आप कल सुरक्षित घर पहुँच गए थे?* |
| `day24_vocab_007` | **Inform** | Verb | `/ɪnˈfɔːm/` | सूचित करना / जानकारी देना | Did he inform the manager about the delay? <br> *क्या उसने देरी के बारे में मैनेजर को सूचित किया था?* |
| `day24_vocab_008` | **Check** | Verb | `/tʃek/` | जाँचना / चेक करना | Did you check your email this morning? <br> *क्या आपने आज सुबह अपना ईमेल चेक किया?* |
| `day24_vocab_009` | **Forget** | Verb | `/fəˈɡet/` | भूलना | Did you forget your laptop charger? <br> *क्या आप अपना लैपटॉप चार्जर भूल गए?* |
| `day24_vocab_010` | **Sleep** | Verb | `/sliːp/` | सोना | Did you sleep well last night? <br> *क्या आप कल रात अच्छी तरह सोए?* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day24_phrase_001` [Social / Polite]: **"Did you sleep well?"** — *क्या आपकी नींद अच्छी रही?*
- `day24_phrase_002` [Conversational]: **"Did you get my message?"** — *क्या आपको मेरा संदेश मिला?*
- `day24_phrase_003` [Social Greeting]: **"Did you have lunch?"** — *क्या आपने दोपहर का खाना खा लिया?*
- `day24_phrase_004` [Formulaic Response]: **"Yes, I did."** — *हाँ, मैंने किया।*
- `day24_phrase_005` [Formulaic Response]: **"No, I didn't."** — *नहीं, मैंने नहीं किया।*
- `day24_phrase_006` [Inquiry]: **"Did anyone call?"** — *क्या किसी का फ़ोन आया था?*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day24_example_001`: **Did you go to the market yesterday?**  
  *क्या तुम कल बाज़ार गए थे?*
- `day24_example_002`: **Did she call you this morning?**  
  *क्या उसने आज सुबह तुम्हें फ़ोन किया था?*
- `day24_example_003`: **Did they enjoy the concert last night?**  
  *क्या उन्हें कल रात कॉन्सर्ट में मज़ा आया?*
- `day24_example_004`: **Did Rahul finish his homework?**  
  *क्या राहुल ने अपना होमवर्क पूरा किया?*
- `day24_example_005`: **Did you buy the fresh milk?**  
  *क्या तुमने ताज़ा दूध खरीदा?*
- `day24_example_006`: **Did the train arrive on time?**  
  *क्या ट्रेन समय पर पहुँची?*
- `day24_example_007`: **Did you find your keys?**  
  *क्या तुम्हें तुम्हारी चाबियाँ मिलीं?*
- `day24_example_008`: **Did we lock the back door?**  
  *क्या हमने पीछे का दरवाज़ा बंद किया था?*
- `day24_example_009`: **Did your parents watch the match?**  
  *क्या आपके माता-पिता ने मैच देखा?*
- `day24_example_010`: **Did you take your medicine on time?**  
  *क्या आपने अपनी दवा समय पर ली?*
- `day24_example_011`: **Did she tell you the good news?**  
  *क्या उसने तुम्हें अच्छी ख़बर सुनाई?*
- `day24_example_012`: **Did you have a good weekend?**  
  *क्या आपका सप्ताहांत अच्छा बीता?*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Rising intonation curve at the end of Yes/No questions (↗), Connected linking /dɪdʒuː/ in 'Did you'
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: In fluent connected speech, 'Did you' naturally blends into /ˈdɪdʒ.uː/ or /dɪdʒə/. Avoid pausing stiffly between 'did' and 'you'.
#### Audio Drills:
- `day24_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "Did you see the movie last night? (↗)"
- `day24_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "Did she call you this morning? (↗)"
- `day24_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "Yes, I did. / No, I didn't."
- `day24_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "Did they arrive safely at the hotel? (↗)"

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (35 Items)
#### 1. `[day24_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PAST_QUESTIONS_YES_NO`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct question to ask 'क्या तुम कल बाज़ार गए थे?':
**Options**: `Did you went to the market yesterday?` | `Did you go to the market yesterday?` | `Do you went to the market yesterday?` | `Were you go to the market yesterday?`
- **Correct Answer**: `Did you go to the market yesterday?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Did' + Subject + Base verb 'go' (V1).
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Did' के साथ मुख्य क्रिया का मूल रूप 'go' (V1) आता है।

#### 2. `[day24_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `SHORT_ANSWERS`)
**Prompt**: What is the natural affirmative short answer to: 'Did you lock the door?'
**Options**: `Yes, I locked.` | `Yes, I did.` | `Yes, I did lock.` | `Yes, I do.`
- **Correct Answer**: `Yes, I did.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Standard short answer is 'Yes, I did.'
- **Explanation (HI)**: प्राकृतिक संक्षिप्त उत्तर 'Yes, I did.' होता है।

#### 3. `[day24_ex_003]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `PAST_QUESTIONS_YES_NO`)
**Prompt**: Complete the question with the base form of (call): 'Did she ______ you yesterday evening?'
- **Correct Answer**: `call`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use base verb 'call' after 'Did she'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Did she' के बाद मूल क्रिया 'call' आएगी।

#### 4. `[day24_ex_004]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PAST_QUESTIONS_YES_NO`)
**Prompt**: Which question correctly avoids the Double Past Error?
**Options**: `Did he saw the doctor?` | `Did he see the doctor?` | `Did he seed the doctor?` | `Did he seen the doctor?`
- **Correct Answer**: `Did he see the doctor?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Did' already carries the past tense, so use base verb 'see'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Did' के साथ क्रिया का पहला रूप 'see' सही है।

#### 5. `[day24_ex_005]` [WORD_REORDER] (medium | `SENTENCE_BUILDING`)
**Prompt**: Arrange into a correct question: [you / breakfast / have / this morning / did]
**Options**: `Did you have breakfast this morning?` | `Did you breakfast have this morning?` | `This morning did have you breakfast?` | `Have you did breakfast this morning?`
- **Correct Answer**: `Did you have breakfast this morning?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Did + Subject (you) + V1 (have) + Object (breakfast) + Time (this morning)?
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: Did you have breakfast this morning?

#### 6. `[day24_ex_006]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_DETECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the mistake: 'Did you bought the train tickets yesterday?'
- **Correct Answer**: `Did you buy the train tickets yesterday?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Bought' must revert to 'buy' in a past question with 'Did'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Did' के कारण 'bought' बदलकर 'buy' होगा।

#### 7. `[day24_ex_007]` [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (easy | `TRANSLATION_HI_EN`)

**Options**: `Did you watch the match last night?` | `Did you watched the match last night?` | `Do you watch the match last night?` | `Were you watch the match last night?`
- **Correct Answer**: `Did you watch the match last night?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Did + you + watch + the match + last night?
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अनुवाद: 'Did you watch the match last night?'

#### 8. `[day24_ex_008]` [ENGLISH_TO_HINDI] (easy | `TRANSLATION_EN_HI`)

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: Two roommates checking with each other before going to bed about evening chores and locks.

#### Dialogue (`day24_dialogue_001`):
- **Siddharth**: "Hey Varun, did you lock the main balcony door?"  
  *(अरे वरुण, क्या तुमने बालकनी का मुख्य दरवाज़ा बंद कर दिया?)*
- **Varun**: "Yes, I did. I locked it right after dinner."  
  *(हाँ, मैंने कर दिया। मैंने डिनर के तुरंत बाद ही ताला लगा दिया था।)*
- **Siddharth**: "Great. Did you turn off the kitchen gas stove as well?"  
  *(बहुत बढ़िया। क्या तुमने किचन का गैस स्टोव भी बंद कर दिया था?)*
- **Varun**: "Yes, I checked it twice. Did you feed the dog?"  
  *(हाँ, मैंने दो बार चेक किया। क्या तुमने कुत्ते को खाना खिला दिया?)*
- **Siddharth**: "No, I didn't! Thanks for reminding me. I'll do it right now."  
  *(नहीं, मैंने नहीं खिलाया! याद दिलाने के लिए शुक्रिया। मैं अभी करता हूँ।)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Ask a partner or friend 3 polite questions about their yesterday using 'Did you sleep well?', 'Did you finish your work?', and 'Did you watch the match?'.
- **HI**: अपने साथी से कल के बारे में 'Did you + V1...?' का उपयोग करके 3 विनम्र प्रश्न पूछें।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day24_assess_001]` **Why is 'Did you watched the movie?' ungrammatical?** ➔ *Because the auxiliary 'did' already carries the past tense marker; the lexical verb must remain in its base form 'watch'.* [Skill: Question Syntax]
- `[day24_assess_002]` **What is the difference between answering 'Yes, I did.' versus '*Yes, I watched*.'?** ➔ *'Yes, I did.' is the standard English formulaic short response using the auxiliary. Lexical verbs are not used alone in standard short replies.* [Skill: Pragmatic Competence]
- `[day24_assess_003]` **How do you ask about someone's location yesterday versus their actions yesterday?** ➔ *Use 'Were you at home?' for past location/state (BE verb) and 'Did you go home?' for past dynamic action.* [Skill: Grammar Discrimination]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `PAST_SIMPLE_QUESTIONS_YES_NO`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14]
