## Day 25: Past Information Questions (WH- Questions with 'Did': Where, When, Why, What time...) {#day-25-day_25}
**शीर्षक**: भूतकाल के सूचनात्मक प्रश्न (WH- शब्द + Did: Where, When, Why, What time...)  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.2` | **Est. Duration**: 30 mins | **Prerequisites**: Day 24 (Past Yes/No Questions & Auxiliary Inversion)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Construct accurate open-ended information questions in the past simple using: 'WH- Word + did + Subject + Base Verb (V1)...?'.
  - **HI**: WH- शब्दों के साथ भूतकाल के विस्तृत प्रश्न बनाना: 'WH- Word + did + Subject + Base Verb (V1)...?'।
- **EN**: Master specific WH- interrogatives in past contexts: Where (place), When / What time (time), Why (reason), What (object/action), How (manner).
  - **HI**: अलग-अलग WH- शब्दों का सही प्रयोग: Where (कहाँ), When / What time (कब/किस समय), Why (क्यों), What (क्या), How (कैसे)।
- **EN**: Maintain strict base verb integrity (V1) after 'did' across all WH- question structures.
  - **HI**: WH- प्रश्नों में भी 'did' के बाद मुख्य क्रिया का मूल रूप (V1) बनाए रखना।
- **EN**: Formulate full natural answers combining past simple affirmative forms with appropriate information.
  - **HI**: भूतकाल के प्रश्नों के उत्तर में V2 क्रियाओं के साथ प्राकृतिक उत्तर देना।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
जब हमें भूतकाल की किसी घटना के बारे में विस्तृत जानकारी चाहिए होती है (जैसे: 'तुम कल कहाँ गए थे?', 'तुमने क्या खरीदा?', 'मीटिंग कब शुरू हुई?', 'उसने नौकरी क्यों छोड़ी?'), तो हम WH- शब्दों (Where, When, What, Why, How, What time) का प्रयोग करते हैं। इसका सटीक सूत्र है: 'WH- Word + did + कर्ता + क्रिया का मूल रूप (V1) + अन्य शब्द?'। यहाँ भी नियम वही रहता है—चूँकि 'did' आ गया है, इसलिए मुख्य क्रिया हमेशा अपने पहले रूप (V1) में रहेगी! उत्तर देते समय चूँकि 'did' हट जाता है, इसलिए क्रिया का दूसरा रूप (V2) आता है (जैसे: Q: 'Where did you go?' ➔ A: 'I went to Delhi.').

**English Explanation**:
To request specific information about completed past events, we form WH- questions using the formula: 'WH- Word + did + Subject + Base Verb (V1) + [Complement]?'. The auxiliary 'did' must always precede the subject, and the lexical verb must strictly remain in its base form (V1). Common WH- words include: What (activity/object), Where (location), When / What time (temporal point), Why (cause/reason), and How (manner/transport). Unlike Yes/No questions, WH- questions use a falling intonation contour at the end.

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: Standard WH- Past Auxiliary Word Order** [Universal Syntax Standard]
  Strict syntactic order: [WH- Question Word] + did + [Subject] + [Base Verb V1] + [Rest of sentence]?
- **Rule 2: Interrogative Function Alignment** [Semantic Mapping]
  Where (Place) | When / What time (Time) | Why (Reason) | What (Object/Action) | How (Manner/Method) | Who did you meet (Person object).
- **Rule 3: Base Verb Requirement in Questions vs. V2 in Answers** [Core Syntax Invariant]
  Question uses V1: 'Where did you stay?' Answer uses V2: 'I stayed at a hotel.' Never use V2 in the question clause (*Where did you stayed?*).
- **Rule 4: Subject Question Exception ('Who called you?')** [Advanced Structural Distinction]
  When 'Who' or 'What' is the grammatical subject (acting as the doer), 'did' is omitted and V2 is used directly: 'Who called you?' (NOT '*Who did call you?*').

#### Master Formula Table:
```json
[
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Location Questions (Where)",
    "formula": "Where + did + [Subject] + [Base Verb V1]?",
    "exampleEn": "Where did you go on vacation?",
    "meaningHi": "तुम छुट्टियों में कहाँ गए थे?"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Temporal Questions (When / What time)",
    "formula": "When / What time + did + [Subject] + [Base Verb V1]?",
    "exampleEn": "What time did the train arrive?",
    "meaningHi": "ट्रेन किस समय पहुँची?"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Reason Questions (Why)",
    "formula": "Why + did + [Subject] + [Base Verb V1]?",
    "exampleEn": "Why did she resign from her job?",
    "meaningHi": "उसने अपनी नौकरी से इस्तीफ़ा क्यों दिया?"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Manner Questions (How)",
    "formula": "How + did + [Subject] + [Base Verb V1]?",
    "exampleEn": "How did you travel to Jaipur?",
    "meaningHi": "आप जयपुर कैसे गए (यात्रा की)?"
  }
]
```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: Omitting auxiliary 'did' ('*Where you went?*') or using double past ('*Where did you went?*')
- **Hindi Pattern**: हिन्दी में 'तुम कहाँ गए?' का सीधा शाब्दिक अनुवाद करके शिक्षार्थी '*Where you went?*' बोल देते हैं, या फिर 'did' लगाने के बाद भी '*Where did you went?*' बोल देते हैं।
- **Potential Interference**: `Saying '*Why you left early?*' or '*What did you bought?*'.`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: English non-subject WH- questions mandatorily require the auxiliary 'did' before the subject, followed by the base verb V1.
- **Natural English Solution**: **Always say 'Where did you go?' and 'Why did you leave early?'.**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day25_vocab_001` | **Arrive** | Verb | `/əˈraɪv/` | पहुँचना | What time did the flight arrive? <br> *फ़्लाइट किस समय पहुँची?* |
| `day25_vocab_002` | **Spend** | Verb | `/spend/` | खर्च करना / बिताना | How much money did you spend on books? <br> *आपने किताबों पर कितने पैसे खर्च किए?* |
| `day25_vocab_003` | **Lose** | Verb | `/luːz/` | खोना / गँवाना | Where did you lose your identity card? <br> *आपका पहचान पत्र कहाँ खो गया?* |
| `day25_vocab_004` | **Choose** | Verb | `/tʃuːz/` | चुनना / पसंद करना | Why did you choose this hotel? <br> *आपने इस होटल को क्यों चुना?* |
| `day25_vocab_005` | **Learn** | Verb | `/lɜːn/` | सीखना | Where did you learn to drive so well? <br> *आपने इतनी अच्छी गाड़ी चलाना कहाँ सीखा?* |
| `day25_vocab_006` | **Return** | Verb | `/rɪˈtɜːn/` | लौटना / वापस आना | When did they return from their holiday? <br> *वे अपनी छुट्टियों से कब वापस लौटे?* |
| `day25_vocab_007` | **Discover** | Verb | `/dɪˈskʌv.ər/` | खोजना / पता लगाना | How did you discover this cafe? <br> *आपको इस कैफ़े का पता कैसे चला?* |
| `day25_vocab_008` | **Order** | Verb | `/ˈɔː.dər/` | ऑर्डर करना / मंगाना | What did you order for lunch? <br> *आपने दोपहर के भोजन के लिए क्या ऑर्डर किया?* |
| `day25_vocab_009` | **Fix** | Verb | `/fɪks/` | ठीक करना / सुधारना | How did the technician fix the computer? <br> *तकनीशियन ने कंप्यूटर कैसे ठीक किया?* |
| `day25_vocab_010` | **Explain** | Verb | `/ɪkˈspleɪn/` | समझाना / व्याख्या करना | What did the doctor explain to you? <br> *डॉक्टर ने आपको क्या समझाया?* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day25_phrase_001` [Conversational Inquiry]: **"What happened?"** — *क्या हुआ?*
- `day25_phrase_002` [Conversational]: **"Where did you go?"** — *तुम कहाँ गए थे?*
- `day25_phrase_003` [Travel Inquiry]: **"When did you arrive?"** — *आप कब पहुँचे?*
- `day25_phrase_004` [Inquiry]: **"Why did you choose that?"** — *आपने वह क्यों चुना?*
- `day25_phrase_005` [Conversational]: **"How did you find it?"** — *आपको यह कैसा लगा / कैसे मिला?*
- `day25_phrase_006` [Time Inquiry]: **"What time did you leave?"** — *आप किस समय निकले?*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day25_example_001`: **Where did you go yesterday?**  
  *तुम कल कहाँ गए थे?*
- `day25_example_002`: **What did you buy at the mall?**  
  *तुमने मॉल में क्या खरीदा?*
- `day25_example_003`: **When did you finish your work?**  
  *तुमने अपना काम कब पूरा किया?*
- `day25_example_004`: **Why did he leave the party early?**  
  *वह पार्टी से जल्दी क्यों चला गया?*
- `day25_example_005`: **How did you travel to Mumbai?**  
  *तुम मुंबई कैसे गए (किस माध्यम से)?*
- `day25_example_006`: **What time did the train arrive?**  
  *ट्रेन किस समय पहुँची?*
- `day25_example_007`: **Who did you meet at the conference?**  
  *आप सम्मेलन में किससे मिले?*
- `day25_example_008`: **How much did this shirt cost?**  
  *इस शर्ट की कीमत कितनी थी?*
- `day25_example_009`: **Where did she stay in Goa?**  
  *वह गोवा में कहाँ रुकी थी?*
- `day25_example_010`: **Why did they cancel the cricket match?**  
  *उन्होंने क्रिकेट मैच क्यों रद्द किया?*
- `day25_example_011`: **What did the manager say in the meeting?**  
  *मैनेजर ने मीटिंग में क्या कहा?*
- `day25_example_012`: **How did you solve this difficult math problem?**  
  *आपने यह कठिन गणित का सवाल कैसे हल किया?*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Falling intonation contour at the end of WH- questions (↘), Rapid unstressed pronunciation of auxiliary 'did you' (/dɪdʒuː/)
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: Do NOT use rising intonation on WH- questions. WH- questions naturally step down in pitch at the end: 'Where did you go? (↘)'.
#### Audio Drills:
- `day25_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "Where did you go yesterday? (↘)"
- `day25_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "What did you buy at the store? (↘)"
- `day25_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "Why did she leave so early? (↘)"
- `day25_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "What time did the train arrive? (↘)"

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (35 Items)
#### 1. `[day25_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `WH_PAST_QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct question to ask 'तुम कल कहाँ गए थे?':
**Options**: `Where you went yesterday?` | `Where did you go yesterday?` | `Where did you went yesterday?` | `Where were you go yesterday?`
- **Correct Answer**: `Where did you go yesterday?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: WH- word (Where) + did + Subject (you) + Base verb (go).
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Where + did + you + go' सही सूत्र है।

#### 2. `[day25_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `WH_PAST_QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Which question correctly asks for the reason of an action?
**Options**: `Why did you leave early?` | `Why you left early?` | `Why did you left early?` | `Why were you leave early?`
- **Correct Answer**: `Why did you leave early?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Why did you leave early?' maintains the base form 'leave' with auxiliary 'did'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Why did you leave' में 'did' के साथ क्रिया का मूल रूप 'leave' है।

#### 3. `[day25_ex_003]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `WH_PAST_QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Complete the question: 'What time ______ the meeting start yesterday?' (Auxiliary verb)
- **Correct Answer**: `did`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `did`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Past question requires auxiliary 'did'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: भूतकाल के प्रश्न में सहायक क्रिया 'did' आएगी।

#### 4. `[day25_ex_004]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `WH_PAST_QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Complete the question with the base form of (buy): 'What did you ______ at the market?'
- **Correct Answer**: `buy`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Base verb 'buy' is used after 'What did you'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'What did you' के बाद मूल क्रिया 'buy' आएगी।

#### 5. `[day25_ex_005]` [WORD_REORDER] (medium | `SENTENCE_BUILDING`)
**Prompt**: Arrange into a correct question: [travel / how / to Goa / did / you]
**Options**: `How did you travel to Goa?` | `How you did travel to Goa?` | `How travel did you to Goa?` | `Did how you travel to Goa?`
- **Correct Answer**: `How did you travel to Goa?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: How + did + you + travel + to Goa?
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: How did you travel to Goa?

#### 6. `[day25_ex_006]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_DETECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'When did you arrived in Delhi?'
- **Correct Answer**: `When did you arrive in Delhi?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Arrived' must be 'arrive' (V1) after 'did'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Did' के कारण 'arrived' बदलकर 'arrive' होगा।

#### 7. `[day25_ex_007]` [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (easy | `TRANSLATION_HI_EN`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'तुमने कल रात क्या खाया?'
**Options**: `What did you eat last night?` | `What did you ate last night?` | `What you ate last night?` | `What were you eat last night?`
- **Correct Answer**: `What did you eat last night?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: What + did + you + eat + last night?
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अनुवाद: 'What did you eat last night?'

#### 8. `[day25_ex_008]` [ENGLISH_TO_HINDI] (easy | `TRANSLATION_EN_HI`)
**Prompt**: What is the Hindi meaning of: 'Why did they cancel the flight?'
**Options**: `उन्होंने फ़्लाइट क्यों रद्द की?` | `फ़्लाइट कब रद्द हुई?` | `क्या फ़्लाइट रद्द हो गई थी?` | `वे फ़्लाइट से क्यों नहीं गए?`
- **Correct Answer**: `उन्होंने फ़्लाइट क्यों रद्द की?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Why did they cancel the flight?' means 'उन्होंने फ़्लाइट क्यों रद्द की?'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अर्थ: 'उन्होंने फ़्लाइट क्यों रद्द की?'

#### 9. `[day25_ex_009]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `WH_PAST_QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Which question word is used to ask about the price of an item in the past?
**Options**: `How much did it cost?` | `How many did it cost?` | `Where did it cost?` | `Why did it cost?`
- **Correct Answer**: `How much did it cost?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'How much did it cost?' asks for the past price.
- **Explanation (HI)**: कीमत पूछने के लिए 'How much did it cost?' का प्रयोग होता है।

#### 10. `[day25_ex_010]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `WH_PAST_QUESTIONS`)

- **Correct Answer**: `stay`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Base verb 'stay'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Did' के बाद 'stay' आएगा।

#### 11. `[day25_ex_011]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (medium | `PHONETICS`)
**Prompt**: What intonation contour is standard for WH- questions?
**Options**: `Falling intonation at the end (↘)` | `Rising intonation at the end (↗)` | `High monotone pitch` | `Screaming`
- **Correct Answer**: `Falling intonation at the end (↘)`
- **Explanation (EN)**: WH- questions naturally fall in pitch at the end.
- **Explanation (HI)**: WH- प्रश्नों के अंत में आवाज़ नीचे गिरती है (Falling Intonation)।

**Options**: `Who did you meet at the conference?` | `Who you did meet at the conference?` | `Who meet did you at the conference?` | `Did you who meet at the conference?`
- **Correct Answer**: `Who did you meet at the conference?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Who + did + you + meet + at the conference?
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: Who did you meet at the conference?

**Prompt**: Fix the error: 'Why did you chose this difficult course?'
- **Correct Answer**: `Why did you choose this difficult course?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Chose' must revert to base form 'choose'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Did' के बाद 'chose' बदलकर 'choose' होगा।

#### 27. `[day25_ex_027]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `WH_PAST_QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank: 'How did the fire ______ (start) in the kitchen?'
- **Correct Answer**: `start`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Base verb 'start'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Did' के बाद 'start' आएगा।

#### 28. `[day25_ex_028]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `WH_PAST_QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Which WH- word correctly completes: '______ did you travel to Kerala? By train or by flight?'
**Options**: `How` | `Why` | `Where` | `What`
- **Correct Answer**: `How`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'How' asks for the method/means of transport.
- **Explanation (HI)**: साधन पूछने के लिए 'How' का प्रयोग होगा।

#### 29. `[day25_ex_029]` [WORD_REORDER] (medium | `SENTENCE_BUILDING`)
**Prompt**: Put in order: [you / order / for dessert / what / did]
**Options**: `What did you order for dessert?` | `What you did order for dessert?` | `What did order you for dessert?` | `Did what you order for dessert?`
- **Correct Answer**: `What did you order for dessert?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: What + did + you + order + for dessert?
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: What did you order for dessert?

#### 30. `[day25_ex_030]` [DIALOGUE_COMPLETION] (easy | `CONVERSATIONAL_USE`)
**Prompt**: Complete the conversation: Maya: 'I went to a bakery yesterday.' Kabir: 'What ______ you buy there?'
- **Correct Answer**: `did`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `did`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use past auxiliary 'did'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: भूतकाल के प्रश्न के लिए 'did' आएगा।

#### 31. `[day25_ex_031]` [SPOKEN_RESPONSE] (easy | `SPEAKING`)
**Prompt**: Speak aloud: Ask someone where they went and what they bought yesterday using 'Where did you go...?' and 'What did you buy...?'.
- **Correct Answer**: `Where did you go [time]? What did you buy [place]?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Construct two open past WH- questions with falling intonation.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Where did you go...?' और 'What did you buy...?' बोलकर दो प्रश्न पूछें।

#### 32. `[day25_ex_032]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Form a past WH- question for: 'She lived in Mumbai.' (Ask about location using 'Where')
- **Correct Answer**: `Where did she live?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `Where did she live`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Where did she live?' with base verb 'live'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Where did she live?' सही प्रश्न है।

#### 33. `[day25_ex_033]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Form a past WH- question for: 'They arrived at 6 PM.' (Ask about time using 'When')
- **Correct Answer**: `When did they arrive?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `When did they arrive`, `What time did they arrive?`, `What time did they arrive`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'When did they arrive?' or 'What time did they arrive?'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'When did they arrive?' सही प्रश्न है।

#### 34. `[day25_ex_034]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Form a past WH- question for: 'He left because he was tired.' (Ask about reason using 'Why')
- **Correct Answer**: `Why did he leave?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `Why did he leave`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Why did he leave?' with base verb 'leave'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Why did he leave?' सही प्रश्न है।

#### 35. `[day25_ex_035]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Form a past WH- question for: 'I bought a new watch.' (Ask about object using 'What')
- **Correct Answer**: `What did you buy?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `What did you buy`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'What did you buy?' with base verb 'buy'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'What did you buy?' सही प्रश्न है।

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: Catching up with a coworker who just returned from an annual vacation in Himachal Pradesh.

#### Dialogue (`day25_dialogue_001`):
- **Aditi**: "Welcome back, Tarun! Where did you go for your vacation?"  
  *(वापसी पर स्वागत है, तरुण! तुम छुट्टियों में कहाँ गए थे?)*
- **Tarun**: "Thanks, Aditi! I went to Manali with my family for five days."  
  *(धन्यवाद, अदिति! मैं अपने परिवार के साथ पाँच दिनों के लिए मनाली गया था।)*
- **Aditi**: "That sounds exciting! How did you travel there?"  
  *(यह तो बहुत रोमांचक लगता है! तुम वहाँ कैसे गए?)*
- **Tarun**: "We took an overnight Volvo bus from Delhi. The mountain drive was scenic."  
  *(हमने दिल्ली से रात की वोल्वो बस ली थी। पहाड़ों का रास्ता बहुत सुंदर था।)*
- **Aditi**: "What activities did you do there, and what did you buy?"  
  *(तुमने वहाँ क्या-क्या किया, और क्या खरीदा?)*
- **Tarun**: "We went trekking, tried local trout fish, and bought warm woolen shawls for our parents."  
  *(हम ट्रैकिंग पर गए, स्थानीय ट्राउट मछली खाई, और अपने माता-पिता के लिए ऊनी शॉल खरीदीं।)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Interview a partner about their last trip using 4 WH- questions: Where did you go? When did you leave? How did you travel? What did you do there?
- **HI**: अपने साथी से उनकी पिछली यात्रा के बारे में 4 WH- प्रश्न पूछें (Where, When, How, What)।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day25_assess_001]` **Why is '*Where you went yesterday?*' incorrect in English?** ➔ *Because English non-subject WH- questions require the auxiliary operator 'did' placed before the subject, followed by the base verb V1 ('Where did you go?').* [Skill: Interrogative Syntax]
- `[day25_assess_002]` **How does the verb form differ between the question 'What did you buy?' and the answer 'I bought a laptop'?** ➔ *The question uses the auxiliary 'did' + base verb 'buy' (V1), whereas the affirmative answer drops 'did' and uses the past simple form 'bought' (V2).* [Skill: Structural Transformation]
- `[day25_assess_003]` **Why is 'What happened?' correct without 'did'?** ➔ *When the question word ('What' or 'Who') acts as the subject/agent of the clause, auxiliary 'did' is omitted and the past tense verb is used directly.* [Skill: Subject Question Mastery]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `PAST_SIMPLE_WH_QUESTIONS`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14]

## Day 26: Past Time Markers & Sequencing Connectors (Yesterday, Last night, Ago, Then, After that) {#day-26-day_26}
**शीर्षक**: भूतकाल के समय सूचक और क्रमबद्ध संयोजक (Yesterday, Last night, Ago, Then, After that)  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.2` | **Est. Duration**: 30 mins | **Prerequisites**: Day 21–25 (Past Simple Full Paradigm)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Differentiate and accurately position past time markers: 'ago' (postposition), 'last' (prepositive with periods), 'yesterday' (parts of day), and 'in + past year'.
  - **HI**: भूतकाल के समय संकेतकों का सही प्रयोग: 'ago' (बाद में), 'last' (पहले), 'yesterday' और 'in + वर्ष'।
- **EN**: Master narrative discourse sequencing connectors: 'First', 'Then', 'Next', 'After that', 'Finally'.
  - **HI**: घटनाओं को क्रमबद्ध ढंग से सुनाने वाले संयोजक (Connectors) सीखना: 'First', 'Then', 'Next', 'After that', 'Finally'।
- **EN**: Recount a multi-stage completed event or daily timeline in coherent chronological order.
  - **HI**: किसी भी घटना या पूरे दिन के घटनाक्रम को सिलसिलेवार अंग्रेज़ी में प्रस्तुत करना।
- **EN**: Eliminate direct Hindi word-order interference like saying '*before two days*' instead of 'two days ago'.
  - **HI**: हिन्दी के 'दो दिन पहले' का गलत अनुवाद '*before two days*' करने की जगह 'two days ago' बोलने की आदत डालना।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
जब हम भूतकाल की कोई घटना या कहानी सुनाते हैं, तो दो चीज़ें बहुत महत्वपूर्ण होती हैं: 1. समय बताना (जैसे: 'तीन दिन पहले', 'पिछले साल', 'कल सुबह') और 2. घटनाओं का सही क्रम बताना (जैसे: 'पहले', 'फिर', 'उसके बाद', 'अंत में')। अंग्रेज़ी में 'समय से पहले' के लिए 'Ago' हमेशा समय के बाद आता है (जैसे: 'two days ago', '*before two days*' गलत है)। इसी तरह 'Last' सीधे समय की अवधि से पहले आता है (जैसे: 'last night', 'last week', 'last year')। कहानी को जोड़ने के लिए हम 'First', 'Then', 'After that' और 'Finally' जैसे कनेक्टर्स का प्रयोग करते हैं।

**English Explanation**:
Narrating past events requires temporal precision and cohesive discourse markers. Past time markers pinpoint completed moments: 'ago' (placed AFTER a duration: 'three days ago'), 'last' (placed BEFORE time periods: 'last week/month/year', 'last night'), 'yesterday' (used with morning/afternoon/evening), and 'in' (with past years: 'in 2021'). To connect actions logically in chronological sequence, English uses discourse sequencing connectors: 'First (of all)', 'Then', 'Next', 'After that', and 'Finally'.

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: The Postpositional Placement of 'Ago'** [Core Syntax Standard]
  'Ago' is placed STRICTLY AFTER the time period: [Number + Time Unit] + ago (e.g., 'two hours ago', 'five years ago'). Never say '*ago two years*' or '*before two years*'.
- **Rule 2: 'Last' vs. 'Yesterday' Prepositionless Collocations** [Idiomatic Collocation]
  Say 'last night', 'last Sunday', 'last month', 'last year'. Say 'yesterday morning', 'yesterday afternoon', 'yesterday evening'. Never say '*yesterday night*' (standard is 'last night'). No preposition ('in/on/at') precedes 'last' or 'yesterday'.
- **Rule 3: Chronological Discourse Sequencing Chain** [Discourse Cohesion]
  Connect chronological events using: First ➔ Then / Next ➔ After that ➔ Finally. Each connector is usually followed by a comma when placed at the head of a clause.
- **Rule 4: Past Year and Specific Date Anchors** [Prepositional Precision]
  Use 'in' for years/months (in 2019, in August) and 'on' for specific calendar dates and days (on May 15th, on Monday).

#### Master Formula Table:
```json
[
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Time Duration + Ago",
    "formula": "[Number] + days/weeks/months/years + ago",
    "exampleEn": "I graduated two years ago.",
    "meaningHi": "मैंने दो साल पहले ग्रेजुएशन किया था।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Last + Time Period",
    "formula": "last + night / week / month / year / [Day of week]",
    "exampleEn": "She bought a car last month.",
    "meaningHi": "उसने पिछले महीने एक कार खरीदी।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Parts of Yesterday",
    "formula": "yesterday + morning / afternoon / evening",
    "exampleEn": "We met yesterday morning.",
    "meaningHi": "हम कल सुबह मिले थे।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Chronological Sequence",
    "formula": "First, [Clause 1]. Then, [Clause 2]. After that, [Clause 3]. Finally, [Clause 4].",
    "exampleEn": "First, I woke up. Then, I had tea. Finally, I left.",
    "meaningHi": "पहले मैं उठा। फिर मैंने चाय पी। अंत में मैं निकल गया।"
  }
]
```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: Using '*before two days*' instead of 'two days ago' and '*yesterday night*' instead of 'last night'
- **Hindi Pattern**: हिन्दी में 'दो दिन पहले' बोलने के कारण भारतीय शिक्षार्थी 'before two days' बोल देते हैं, और 'कल रात' का सीधा अनुवाद 'yesterday night' कर देते हैं।
- **Potential Interference**: `Saying '*I came here before two days*' or '*Yesterday night I watched TV*'.`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: 'Ago' is measured backward from the present moment. 'Before' requires a relative reference point ('before the meeting'). 'Last night' is the fixed idiomatic expression in English.
- **Natural English Solution**: **Always say 'two days ago' and 'last night'.**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day26_vocab_001` | **Ago** | Adverb | `/əˈɡəʊ/` | पहले (समय की अवधि के बाद) | The train departed ten minutes ago. <br> *ट्रेन दस मिनट पहले रवाना हुई थी।* |
| `day26_vocab_002` | **Yesterday** | Adverb/Noun | `/ˈjes.tə.deɪ/` | बीता हुआ कल | We visited the museum yesterday afternoon. <br> *हमने कल दोपहर संग्रहालय का दौरा किया।* |
| `day26_vocab_003` | **Previous** | Adjective | `/ˈpriː.vi.əs/` | पिछला / पूर्व | He discussed the previous meeting's decisions. <br> *उसने पिछली बैठक के निर्णयों पर चर्चा की।* |
| `day26_vocab_004` | **Last** | Determiner/Adverb | `/lɑːst/` | पिछला / अंतिम | They moved to Bangalore last year. <br> *वे पिछले साल बैंगलोर चले गए।* |
| `day26_vocab_005` | **Finally** | Adverb | `/ˈfaɪ.nəl.i/` | आखिरकार / अंत में | Finally, the rain stopped and we went out. <br> *आखिरकार बारिश रुक गई और हम बाहर गए।* |
| `day26_vocab_006` | **Afterwards** | Adverb | `/ˈɑːf.tə.wədz/` | इसके बाद / बाद में | We had dinner and watched a movie afterwards. <br> *हमने रात का खाना खाया और उसके बाद एक फ़िल्म देखी।* |
| `day26_vocab_007` | **Suddenly** | Adverb | `/ˈsʌd.ən.li/` | अचानक | Suddenly, the electricity went off. <br> *अचानक बिजली चली गई।* |
| `day26_vocab_008` | **Eventually** | Adverb | `/ɪˈven.tʃu.ə.li/` | अंततः / समय बीतने पर | Eventually, they reached their hotel safely. <br> *अंततः वे सुरक्षित अपने होटल पहुँच गए।* |
| `day26_vocab_009` | **Earlier** | Adverb | `/ˈɜː.li.ər/` | पहले / कुछ देर पहले | I saw him earlier this morning. <br> *मैंने उसे आज सुबह कुछ देर पहले देखा था।* |
| `day26_vocab_010` | **Recently** | Adverb | `/ˈriː.sənt.li/` | हाल ही में | She recently joined a new software firm. <br> *वह हाल ही में एक नई सॉफ्टवेयर कंपनी में शामिल हुई।* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day26_phrase_001` [Time Expression]: **"Two days ago"** — *दो दिन पहले*
- `day26_phrase_002` [Discourse Marker]: **"First of all"** — *सबसे पहले*
- `day26_phrase_003` [Connector]: **"After that"** — *उसके बाद*
- `day26_phrase_004` [Narrative Transition]: **"Then suddenly"** — *फिर अचानक*
- `day26_phrase_005` [Narrative Conclusion]: **"Finally, we reached home."** — *आखिरकार हम घर पहुँच गए।*
- `day26_phrase_006` [Time Expression]: **"Earlier this morning"** — *आज सुबह कुछ देर पहले*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day26_example_001`: **I finished my degree three years ago.**  
  *मैंने तीन साल पहले अपनी डिग्री पूरी की थी।*
- `day26_example_002`: **We had a family dinner last night.**  
  *हमने कल रात पारिवारिक डिनर किया।*
- `day26_example_003`: **First, I woke up at six. Then, I went for a run.**  
  *पहले मैं छह बजे उठा। फिर मैं दौड़ने गया।*
- `day26_example_004`: **After that, we had breakfast and left for the office.**  
  *उसके बाद हमने नाश्ता किया और दफ़्तर के लिए निकल गए।*
- `day26_example_005`: **Suddenly, the phone rang and woke me up.**  
  *अचानक फ़ोन बजा और मेरी नींद खुल गई।*
- `day26_example_006`: **She arrived in Mumbai two weeks ago.**  
  *वह दो हफ़्ते पहले मुंबई पहुँची।*
- `day26_example_007`: **They bought this house in 2020.**  
  *उन्होंने यह घर 2020 में खरीदा था।*
- `day26_example_008`: **Finally, after three hours of waiting, the train arrived.**  
  *आखिरकार तीन घंटे के इंतज़ार के बाद ट्रेन आ गई।*
- `day26_example_009`: **We met him yesterday afternoon at the bookstore.**  
  *हम कल दोपहर उससे किताबों की दुकान पर मिले।*
- `day26_example_010`: **First, check the document. Next, sign on the last page.**  
  *पहले दस्तावेज़ की जाँच करें। इसके बाद अंतिम पृष्ठ पर हस्ताक्षर करें।*
- `day26_example_011`: **I saw an old friend earlier today.**  
  *मैंने आज कुछ देर पहले एक पुराने दोस्त को देखा था।*
- `day26_example_012`: **He called his parents last Sunday.**  
  *उसने पिछले रविवार अपने माता-पिता को फ़ोन किया।*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Rhythm and pause phrasing after sentence-initial connectors (First, / Then, / After that, / Finally,), Unstressed schwa in 'ago' /əˈɡəʊ/
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: Do not stress 'a-' in 'ago'. It is a weak schwa /ə/. Make sure to insert a slight natural pause after introductory sequencing connectors.
#### Audio Drills:
- `day26_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "Two days ago, I called my manager."
- `day26_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "First, we had tea. Then, we left the hotel."
- `day26_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "After that, we visited the Taj Mahal."
- `day26_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "Finally, we reached Delhi last night."

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (35 Items)
#### 1. `[day26_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `TIME_MARKERS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct sentence to say 'मैं दो दिन पहले आया था':
**Options**: `I arrived before two days.` | `I arrived two days ago.` | `I arrived two days before.` | `I arrived ago two days.`
- **Correct Answer**: `I arrived two days ago.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Ago' is placed directly after the time duration: 'two days ago'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: समय अवधि के बाद 'ago' आता है: 'two days ago' सही है।

#### 2. `[day26_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `TIME_MARKERS`)
**Prompt**: Which expression is the standard idiomatic English for 'कल रात'?
**Options**: `Yesterday night` | `Last night` | `In last night` | `Yesterday evening night`
- **Correct Answer**: `Last night`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Last night' is the fixed standard English expression.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'कल रात' के लिए मानक अंग्रेज़ी 'Last night' है।

#### 3. `[day26_ex_003]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `SEQUENCING_CONNECTORS`)
**Prompt**: Complete the sentence with the correct sequencing word: 'First, I washed the car. ______, I cleaned the garage.'
- **Correct Answer**: `Then`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `then`, `Next`, `next`, `After that`, `after that`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Then' or 'Next' or 'After that' follows 'First' in chronological sequence.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'First' के बाद 'Then' या 'After that' आता है।

#### 4. `[day26_ex_004]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PREPOSITIONS_OF_TIME`)
**Prompt**: Which preposition is used with a specific past year (e.g. 2021)?
**Options**: `On 2021` | `At 2021` | `In 2021` | `Ago 2021`
- **Correct Answer**: `In 2021`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Years take the preposition 'in': 'in 2021'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: वर्षों के साथ 'in' का प्रयोग होता है: 'in 2021'।

#### 5. `[day26_ex_005]` [WORD_REORDER] (medium | `SENTENCE_BUILDING`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the chronological sequence: [reached home / we / after that / finally / then / had dinner / we]
**Options**: `First we had dinner, and finally we reached home.` | `Finally had dinner we then reached home.` | `Reached home we after that finally had dinner.` | `Then finally we had dinner reached home.`
- **Correct Answer**: `First we had dinner, and finally we reached home.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Logical narrative flow: First [Action 1], and finally [Action 2].
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: First we had dinner, and finally we reached home.

- **Correct Answer**: `I met him three months ago in Mumbai.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Before three months' must be corrected to 'three months ago'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Before three months' की जगह 'three months ago' होगा।

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: Narrating an eventful yesterday to a friend, explaining how an unexpected power outage disrupted dinner.

#### Dialogue (`day26_dialogue_001`):
- **Deepak**: "How was your evening yesterday, Manisha?"  
  *(मनीषा, कल तुम्हारी शाम कैसी रही?)*
- **Manisha**: "It was quite eventful! First, I reached home at seven and started cooking dinner."  
  *(काफी हलचल भरी रही! पहले, मैं सात बजे घर पहुँची और रात का खाना पकाना शुरू किया।)*
- **Deepak**: "And then what happened?"  
  *(और फिर क्या हुआ?)*
- **Manisha**: "Then suddenly, around eight o'clock, the entire neighborhood lost power! It was completely dark."  
  *(फिर अचानक, लगभग आठ बजे, पूरे इलाके की बिजली चली गई! पूरी तरह अंधेरा छा गया।)*
- **Deepak**: "Oh no! How did you manage?"  
  *(अरे नहीं! फिर तुमने कैसे संभाला?)*
- **Manisha**: "After that, we lit some candles and ordered pizza. Finally, the electricity returned at midnight!"  
  *(उसके बाद, हमने मोमबत्तियाँ जलाईं और पिज़्ज़ा मंगाया। आखिरकार, आधी रात को बिजली वापस आ गई!)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Tell a 4-sentence chronological story about yesterday using: 'First, ... Then, ... After that, ... Finally, ...'.
- **HI**: 'First...', 'Then...', 'After that...', 'Finally...' का उपयोग करके कल के बारे में 4 वाक्यों की एक सिलसिलेवार कहानी बोलें।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day26_assess_001]` **Why is 'I met him before two days' incorrect in English?** ➔ *'Ago' is the mandatory postposition used to measure time back from the present moment: 'two days ago'. 'Before' requires a specific relative event (e.g. 'before the lunch').* [Skill: Temporal Syntax]
- `[day26_assess_002]` **Why do we say 'last night' instead of 'yesterday night'?** ➔ *'Last night' is the fixed lexical collocation in English. 'Yesterday' is paired with morning, afternoon, and evening, but never with night.* [Skill: Collocational Accuracy]
- `[day26_assess_003]` **List 4 sequencing connectors in their natural chronological order.** ➔ *First (of all) ➔ Then / Next ➔ After that / Afterwards ➔ Finally.* [Skill: Discourse Structuring]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `PAST_TIME_MARKERS_AND_SEQUENCING`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14]

## Day 27: Giving Reasons & Explanations in Past (Because, So, That's why & Cause-Effect) {#day-27-day_27}
**शीर्षक**: भूतकाल में कारण और परिणाम बताना (Because, So, That's why और Cause-Effect)  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.2` | **Est. Duration**: 30 mins | **Prerequisites**: Day 26 (Past Time Markers & Connectors)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Differentiate cause clauses ('because + Reason') from result/effect clauses ('so / that's why + Result').
  - **HI**: कारण बताने वाले उपवाक्य ('because + कारण') और परिणाम बताने वाले उपवाक्य ('so / that's why + परिणाम') में अंतर समझना।
- **EN**: Maintain consistent past tense agreement across both clauses in compound sentences.
  - **HI**: दोनों उपवाक्यों में भूतकाल की संगति (Tense Agreement) बनाए रखना।
- **EN**: Explain reasons for past absences, delays, cancellations, and decisions politely in personal and workplace contexts.
  - **HI**: काम पर देर से आने, अनुपस्थित रहने या योजनाओं के बदलने के कारणों को विनम्रतापूर्वक स्पष्ट करना।
- **EN**: Eliminate double-conjunction errors like using '*Because... so...*' in the same English sentence.
  - **HI**: हिन्दी के 'चूँकि... इसलिए...' का गलत अंग्रेज़ी अनुवाद '*Because... so...*' एक साथ बोलने की गलती से बचना।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
जब हम भूतकाल की किसी घटना का 'कारण' (Reason) या उसका 'परिणाम' (Result/Effect) बताते हैं, तो हम दो मुख्य शब्दों का प्रयोग करते हैं: 1. 'Because' (क्योंकि) – यह कारण बताता है (जैसे: 'मैं नहीं आया क्योंकि मैं बीमार था' ➔ 'I didn't come because I was sick')। 2. 'So' (इसलिए / तो) – यह परिणाम बताता है (जैसे: 'मैं बीमार था, इसलिए मैं नहीं आया' ➔ 'I was sick, so I didn't come')। सबसे बड़ा नियम: हिन्दी में हम 'चूँकि... इसलिए...' दोनों एक साथ बोलते हैं, लेकिन अंग्रेज़ी के एक वाक्य में 'Because' और 'So' दोनों एक साथ कभी नहीं आते! या तो सिर्फ 'Because' का प्रयोग करें, या सिर्फ 'So' का!

**English Explanation**:
Explaining causes and consequences in past narratives requires two complementary conjunctions: 'Because' introduces the CAUSE / REASON (Result + because + Reason: 'She was late because the train broke down'). 'So' or 'That's why' introduces the EFFECT / RESULT (Reason + so + Result: 'The train broke down, so she was late'). Tense agreement is essential: both clauses must maintain past forms (was/were, didn't, V2). Crucially, English forbids combining 'Because' and 'So' in the same sentence (saying '*Because I was sick, so I stayed home*' is ungrammatical).

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: Clause Structure with 'Because' (Reason Focus)** [Causal Conjunction]
  [Main Clause / Result] + because + [Reason Clause]. Example: 'I went to bed early because I was exhausted.' 'Because' explains 'WHY'.
- **Rule 2: Clause Structure with 'So' (Result Focus)** [Consecutive Conjunction]
  [Reason Clause] + , so + [Result Clause]. Example: 'I was exhausted, so I went to bed early.' A comma precedes 'so' when joining two independent clauses.
- **Rule 3: The Single Conjunction Mandate (No '*Because... so*')** [Core Syntax Invariant]
  Never use 'Because' and 'So' together in one sentence. Correct: 'Because it was raining, we stayed indoors.' OR 'It was raining, so we stayed indoors.' Never say: '*Because it was raining, so we stayed indoors.*'
- **Rule 4: Inter-Clause Tense Harmony** [Tense Agreement]
  Maintain past tense throughout both clauses: 'I didn't buy the phone (past) because it was (past) too expensive.' Do not mix present is/are unless stating a timeless universal fact.

#### Master Formula Table:
```json
[
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Reason Pattern (because)",
    "formula": "[Result: Subject + Past Verb] + because + [Reason: Subject + Past Verb]",
    "exampleEn": "He missed the bus because he woke up late.",
    "meaningHi": "उसकी बस छूट गई क्योंकि वह देर से उठा।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Result Pattern (so)",
    "formula": "[Reason: Subject + Past Verb] + , so + [Result: Subject + Past Verb]",
    "exampleEn": "He woke up late, so he missed the bus.",
    "meaningHi": "वह देर से उठा, इसलिए उसकी बस छूट गई।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Explanatory Pattern (That's why)",
    "formula": "[Reason: Subject + Past Verb]. That's why + [Result: Subject + Past Verb].",
    "exampleEn": "I lost my keys. That's why I called you.",
    "meaningHi": "मेरी चाबियाँ खो गई थीं। इसीलिए मैंने तुम्हें फ़ोन किया।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Initial Reason Pattern",
    "formula": "Because + [Reason Clause], + [Result Clause]",
    "exampleEn": "Because it rained heavily, the match was canceled.",
    "meaningHi": "क्योंकि भारी बारिश हुई, मैच रद्द कर दिया गया।"
  }
]
```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: The Double Conjunction Error ('*Because... so...*') and tense mismatch
- **Hindi Pattern**: हिन्दी में 'चूँकि बारिश हो रही थी, इसलिए हम नहीं गए' में दोनों शब्द आते हैं, जिससे शिक्षार्थी अंग्रेज़ी में '*Because it was raining, so we didn't go*' बोल देते हैं।
- **Potential Interference**: `Saying '*Because I was tired, so I slept*' or '*I didn't go because I am sick*'.`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: English grammar allows only ONE coordinating or subordinating conjunction to connect two clauses. Subordinating 'because' already establishes the relationship.
- **Natural English Solution**: **Say 'Because I was tired, I slept' OR 'I was tired, so I slept'.**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day27_vocab_001` | **Because** | Conjunction | `/bɪˈkɒz/` | क्योंकि / चूँकि | I stayed home because I felt sick. <br> *मैं घर पर ही रुका क्योंकि मेरी तबीयत खराब लग रही थी।* |
| `day27_vocab_002` | **Reason** | Noun | `/ˈriː.zən/` | कारण / वजह | He explained the reason for the delay. <br> *उसने देरी की वजह बताई।* |
| `day27_vocab_003` | **Result** | Noun | `/rɪˈzʌlt/` | परिणाम / नतीजा | As a result, they won the match. <br> *नतीजतन, वे मैच जीत गए।* |
| `day27_vocab_004` | **Therefore** | Adverb | `/ˈðeə.fɔːr/` | अतः / इसलिए (Formal) | He worked hard; therefore, he succeeded. <br> *उसने कड़ी मेहनत की; अतः वह सफल हुआ।* |
| `day27_vocab_005` | **Cancel** | Verb | `/ˈkæn.səl/` | रद्द करना | They canceled the meeting due to bad weather. <br> *उन्होंने खराब मौसम के कारण बैठक रद्द कर दी।* |
| `day27_vocab_006` | **Delay** | Noun/Verb | `/dɪˈleɪ/` | देरी / देर होना | The flight was delayed by two hours. <br> *फ़्लाइट में दो घंटे की देरी हुई थी।* |
| `day27_vocab_007` | **Miss** | Verb | `/mɪs/` | छूट जाना (ट्रेन/बस) | I missed the morning train because of traffic. <br> *ट्रैफ़िक के कारण मेरी सुबह की ट्रेन छूट गई।* |
| `day27_vocab_008` | **Forget** | Verb | `/fəˈɡet/` | भूलना | She forgot her password, so she couldn't log in. <br> *वह अपना पासवर्ड भूल गई थी, इसलिए वह लॉग इन नहीं कर सकी।* |
| `day27_vocab_009` | **Repair** | Verb | `/rɪˈpeər/` | मरम्मत करना / सुधारना | The mechanic repaired my bike yesterday. <br> *मैकेनिक ने कल मेरी बाइक की मरम्मत की।* |
| `day27_vocab_010` | **Cause** | Noun/Verb | `/kɔːz/` | कारण बनना / वजह | Heavy rain caused waterlogging in the city. <br> *भारी बारिश के कारण शहर में जलभराव हो गया।* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day27_phrase_001` [Conversational]: **"That's why I called you."** — *इसीलिए मैंने तुम्हें फ़ोन किया।*
- `day27_phrase_002` [Explanation]: **"Because the train was late"** — *क्योंकि ट्रेन लेट थी*
- `day27_phrase_003` [Conversational]: **"I was exhausted, so I rested."** — *मैं बहुत थक गया था, इसलिए मैंने आराम किया।*
- `day27_phrase_004` [Conversational]: **"There was heavy traffic, so we walked."** — *भारी ट्रैफ़िक था, इसलिए हम पैदल चले।*
- `day27_phrase_005` [Prepositional Phrase]: **"Because of the rain"** — *बारिश की वजह से*
- `day27_phrase_006` [Formal / Neutral]: **"That is the main reason."** — *यही मुख्य कारण है।*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day27_example_001`: **I didn't come to office because I had a fever.**  
  *मैं दफ़्तर नहीं आया क्योंकि मुझे बुख़ार था।*
- `day27_example_002`: **It was raining heavily, so we stayed indoors.**  
  *भारी बारिश हो रही थी, इसलिए हम घर के अंदर ही रहे।*
- `day27_example_003`: **She didn't buy the dress because it was too expensive.**  
  *उसने वह ड्रेस नहीं खरीदी क्योंकि वह बहुत महंगी थी।*
- `day27_example_004`: **The bus broke down, so we took a taxi.**  
  *बस खराब हो गई, इसलिए हमने टैक्सी ले ली।*
- `day27_example_005`: **He forgot his wallet, so he couldn't pay the bill.**  
  *वह अपना बटुआ भूल गया, इसलिए वह बिल नहीं चुका सका।*
- `day27_example_006`: **Because the internet was down, we couldn't send the report.**  
  *क्योंकि इंटरनेट बंद था, हम रिपोर्ट नहीं भेज सके।*
- `day27_example_007`: **I lost my umbrella. That's why I got wet in the rain.**  
  *मेरा छाता खो गया था। इसीलिए मैं बारिश में भीग गया।*
- `day27_example_008`: **They arrived late because of heavy traffic on the highway.**  
  *वे हाईवे पर भारी ट्रैफ़िक के कारण देर से पहुँचे।*
- `day27_example_009`: **He studied very hard, so he passed the examination.**  
  *उसने बहुत मेहनत से पढ़ाई की, इसलिए उसने परीक्षा पास कर ली।*
- `day27_example_010`: **She didn't eat lunch because she was not hungry.**  
  *उसने दोपहर का खाना नहीं खाया क्योंकि उसे भूख नहीं थी।*
- `day27_example_011`: **The shop was closed, so I went to another supermarket.**  
  *दुकान बंद थी, इसलिए मैं दूसरे सुपरमार्केट गया।*
- `day27_example_012`: **I was tired yesterday evening, so I slept at nine.**  
  *कल शाम मैं थका हुआ था, इसलिए मैं नौ बजे सो गया।*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Unstressed /bɪˈkɒz/ or conversational /kəz/, Pause before conjunction 'so' /soʊ/ with slight comma intonation
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: Do not pronounce 'because' as 'bee-cause' with heavy stress on the first syllable. In natural speech, the first syllable is reduced to /bɪ/ or /bə/.
#### Audio Drills:
- `day27_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "I was tired, so I went to sleep."
- `day27_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "She stayed home because she had a fever."
- `day27_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "The train was late. That's why I called you."
- `day27_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "Because it rained, the match was delayed."

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (35 Items)
#### 1. `[day27_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `CAUSAL_CONNECTORS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct sentence to say 'मैं नहीं आया क्योंकि मैं बीमार था':
**Options**: `I didn't come because I was sick.` | `Because I was sick so I didn't come.` | `I didn't come so I was sick.` | `I was sick because I didn't come.`
- **Correct Answer**: `I didn't come because I was sick.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Because' introduces the reason ('I was sick').
- **Explanation (HI)**: कारण बताने के लिए 'because' का प्रयोग होता है।

#### 2. `[day27_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `RESULT_CONNECTORS`)
**Prompt**: Which sentence correctly uses 'so' to show result?
**Options**: `The bus broke down, so we took a taxi.` | `The bus broke down because we took a taxi.` | `Because the bus broke down, so we took a taxi.` | `We took a taxi, so the bus broke down.`
- **Correct Answer**: `The bus broke down, so we took a taxi.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'So' introduces the result ('we took a taxi').
- **Explanation (HI)**: परिणाम बताने के लिए 'so' का प्रयोग होता है।

#### 3. `[day27_ex_003]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `ERROR_DETECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the mistake: 'Because I was tired, so I slept early.'
- **Correct Answer**: `Because I was tired, I slept early.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Never combine 'Because' and 'So' in the same sentence. Remove 'so'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Because' और 'so' दोनों एक साथ नहीं आते। 'So' हटाएँ।

- **Explanation (HI)**: ड्राइविंग करना कारण था, इसलिए 'because' आएगा।

#### 5. `[day27_ex_005]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `RESULT_CONNECTORS`)

- **Correct Answer**: `I lost my wallet. That's why I called you.`

- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: I lost my wallet. That's why I called you.

**Prompt**: Fill in the blank: 'We reached safely ______ the driver drove very carefully.' (because / so)
- **Correct Answer**: `because`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'The driver drove carefully' is the reason.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सावधानी से गाड़ी चलाना कारण था, इसलिए 'because' आएगा।

#### 29. `[day27_ex_029]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (medium | `CLAUSE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Which sentence explains the reason FIRST?
**Options**: `Because the laptop was slow, I restarted it.` | `I restarted the laptop because it was slow.` | `I restarted the laptop, so it was slow.` | `The laptop was slow because I restarted it.`
- **Correct Answer**: `Because the laptop was slow, I restarted it.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Starting with 'Because' places the reason clause at the beginning.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Because' से शुरू करने पर कारण पहले आ जाता है।

#### 30. `[day27_ex_030]` [WORD_REORDER] (medium | `SENTENCE_BUILDING`)
**Prompt**: Reorder: [forgot my glasses / couldn't read / I / so / I / the newspaper]
**Options**: `I forgot my glasses, so I couldn't read the newspaper.` | `So I forgot my glasses I couldn't read the newspaper.` | `I couldn't read the newspaper so forgot I my glasses.` | `Forgot I my glasses so couldn't read I the newspaper.`
- **Correct Answer**: `I forgot my glasses, so I couldn't read the newspaper.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Reason + , so + Result.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: I forgot my glasses, so I couldn't read the newspaper.

#### 31. `[day27_ex_031]` [DIALOGUE_COMPLETION] (easy | `CONVERSATIONAL_USE`)
**Prompt**: Complete the conversation: Boss: 'Why were you absent yesterday, Priya?' Priya: 'I had a severe migraine, ______ (because / so / that's why) I couldn't come to office.'
- **Correct Answer**: `so`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `that's why`, `so`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'So' connects the migraine (reason) to absence (result).
- **Explanation (HI)**: परिणाम जोड़ने के लिए 'so' या 'that's why' आएगा।

#### 32. `[day27_ex_032]` [SPOKEN_RESPONSE] (easy | `SPEAKING`)
**Prompt**: Speak aloud: Give a reason why you were late or stayed home using '...because...' or '..., so...'.
- **Correct Answer**: `[Reason clause], so [Result clause]. / [Result clause] because [Reason clause].`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Construct a compound sentence explaining a cause-and-effect relationship in past tense.
- **Explanation (HI)**: भूतकाल में कारण और परिणाम बताते हुए एक वाक्य बोलें।

#### 33. `[day27_ex_033]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Which conjunction introduces a REASON ('क्योंकि')?
- **Correct Answer**: `because`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `Because`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Because' introduces reasons.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Because' कारण बताता है।

#### 34. `[day27_ex_034]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Which conjunction introduces a RESULT ('इसलिए')?
- **Correct Answer**: `so`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `So`, `that's why`, `That's why`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'So' introduces results.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'So' परिणाम बताता है।

#### 35. `[day27_ex_035]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Can 'Because' and 'So' be used together in the same English sentence?
- **Correct Answer**: `No`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `no`, `never`, `No, never`
- **Explanation (EN)**: No, never combine 'Because' and 'So' in one sentence.
- **Explanation (HI)**: नहीं, 'Because' और 'So' एक साथ कभी नहीं आते।

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: Explaining to a team lead why an urgent email was delayed during yesterday's office internet outage.

#### Dialogue (`day27_dialogue_001`):
- **Neeraj**: "Good morning, Sanya. Did you send the client quotation yesterday?"  
  *(शुभ प्रभात, सान्या। क्या तुमने कल क्लाइंट को कोटेशन भेजा था?)*
- **Sanya**: "I prepared it on time, but our office Wi-Fi disconnected at 4 PM, so I couldn't email it."  
  *(मैंने समय पर तैयार कर लिया था, लेकिन शाम 4 बजे हमारे ऑफिस का वाई-फ़ाई डिस्कनेक्ट हो गया, इसलिए मैं ईमेल नहीं भेज सकी।)*
- **Neeraj**: "I understand. Did you try using your phone hotspot?"  
  *(मैं समझता हूँ। क्या तुमने अपने फ़ोन के हॉटस्पॉट का इस्तेमाल करने की कोशिश की?)*
- **Sanya**: "I tried, but my phone battery died because I forgot my charger at home. That's why I couldn't connect."  
  *(मैंने कोशिश की, लेकिन मेरे फ़ोन की बैटरी खत्म हो गई क्योंकि मैं अपना चार्जर घर पर भूल गई थी। इसीलिए मैं कनेक्ट नहीं कर सकी।)*
- **Neeraj**: "No problem. Please send it right away this morning."  
  *(कोई बात नहीं। कृपया आज सुबह इसे तुरंत भेज दो।)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Explain a problem you faced recently and give the reason and result using '...because...' and '..., so...'.
- **HI**: हाल ही में आई किसी समस्या का कारण और परिणाम 'because' और 'so' का उपयोग करके बताएं।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day27_assess_001]` **Why is 'Because I was sick, so I didn't come' considered a major grammatical error?** ➔ *Because English does not allow two coordinating/subordinating conjunctions to connect the same two clauses. Use either 'Because I was sick, I didn't come' OR 'I was sick, so I didn't come'.* [Skill: Conjunction Syntax]
- `[day27_assess_002]` **How does the focus differ between 'I stayed home because I was tired' and 'I was tired, so I stayed home'?** ➔ *'Because' places focus on the underlying cause/reason, whereas 'so' places focus on the resulting consequence/outcome.* [Skill: Discourse Semantics]
- `[day27_assess_003]` **Explain the rule of inter-clause tense harmony when giving reasons for past events.** ➔ *Both the main result clause and the causal reason clause must agree in past tense forms (e.g. 'didn't buy' with 'was', NOT with present 'is').* [Skill: Tense Harmony]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `PAST_CAUSAL_AND_RESULT_CLAUSES`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14]

## Day 28: Past Continuous Basics (Was/Were + Verb-ing & Ongoing Past Actions) {#day-28-day_28}
**शीर्षक**: भूतकाल निरंतर काल की बुनियादी बातें (Was/Were + Verb-ing और जारी भूतकालीन कार्य)  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.2` | **Est. Duration**: 30 mins | **Prerequisites**: Day 21–27 (Past Simple Full Paradigm)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Form accurate Past Continuous sentences using: 'Subject + was/were + Verb-ing'.
  - **HI**: Past Continuous (अपूर्ण भूतकाल) के सटीक वाक्य बनाना: 'Subject + was/were + Verb-ing'।
- **EN**: Select appropriate auxiliary based on subject number (I/He/She/It + was; You/We/They + were).
  - **HI**: कर्ता के अनुसार सही सहायक क्रिया चुनना (I/He/She/It के साथ 'was'; You/We/They के साथ 'were')।
- **EN**: Describe activities that were ongoing/in-progress at a specific past point in time (e.g., 'At 8 PM yesterday, I was having dinner').
  - **HI**: भूतकाल के किसी निश्चित समय पर जारी चल रहे कार्यों का वर्णन करना (जैसे: 'कल रात 8 बजे मैं खाना खा रहा था')।
- **EN**: Construct negative and interrogative forms ('wasn't / weren't + V-ing' & 'Was / Were you + V-ing?').
  - **HI**: नकारात्मक और प्रश्नवाचक वाक्य बनाना ('wasn't/weren't + V-ing' और 'Was/Were you + V-ing?')।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
जब हमें बताना हो कि भूतकाल के किसी निश्चित समय पर कोई काम 'चल रहा था' या 'जारी था' (जैसे: 'रहा था', 'रही थी', 'रहे थे' — 'मैं कल शाम टीवी देख रहा था', 'वे क्रिकेट खेल रहे थे'), तो हम Past Continuous Tense का प्रयोग करते हैं। इसका सूत्र है: 'Subject + was / were + Verb-ing'। ध्यान रखें: I, He, She, It और एकवचन संज्ञा के साथ 'was' आता है; You, We, They और बहुवचन संज्ञा के साथ 'were' आता है। नकारात्मक बनाने के लिए 'was not (wasn't)' या 'were not (weren't)' का प्रयोग करते हैं।

**English Explanation**:
The Past Continuous (Past Progressive) tense describes actions that were actively in progress at a specific temporal reference point in the past. Formula: 'Subject + was/were + Base Verb-ing'. Subject Concord: Use 'was' with singular subjects (I, he, she, it, singular nouns); use 'were' with plural subjects and second person (you, we, they, plural nouns). Negation: 'wasn't / weren't + Verb-ing'. Questions invert the auxiliary: 'Was/Were + Subject + Verb-ing?'.

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: Subject-Auxiliary Concord (Was vs. Were)** [Core Syntax Standard]
  I / He / She / It / Singular Noun ➔ WAS | You / We / They / Plural Nouns ➔ WERE. Never say '*You was*' or '*They was*'.
- **Rule 2: Present Participle (-ing) Suffixation Rules** [Morphological Precision]
  Base verb + ing (read ➔ reading; write ➔ writing; run ➔ running; die ➔ dying).
- **Rule 3: Ongoing Temporal Reference Point Function** [Aspectual Semantics]
  Expresses duration/progress at an exact past moment: 'At 7 PM yesterday, I was studying.' Action started before 7 PM and was still happening.
- **Rule 4: Stative Verb Exception** [Aspectual Constraint]
  Non-action/stative verbs (know, want, like, belong, need) are NOT used in continuous forms. Say 'I wanted help' (NOT '*I was wanting help*').

#### Master Formula Table:
```json
[
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Singular Affirmative (I, He, She, It)",
    "formula": "Subject + was + Verb-ing + [Object]",
    "exampleEn": "He was sleeping at midnight.",
    "meaningHi": "वह आधी रात को सो रहा था।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Plural Affirmative (You, We, They)",
    "formula": "Subject + were + Verb-ing + [Object]",
    "exampleEn": "They were playing football.",
    "meaningHi": "वे फ़ुटबॉल खेल रहे थे।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Negative Statement",
    "formula": "Subject + wasn't / weren't + Verb-ing",
    "exampleEn": "I wasn't watching television.",
    "meaningHi": "मैं टेलीविज़न नहीं देख रहा था।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Interrogative (Yes/No Question)",
    "formula": "Was / Were + Subject + Verb-ing?",
    "exampleEn": "Were you studying at 10 PM?",
    "meaningHi": "क्या तुम रात 10 बजे पढ़ाई कर रहे थे?"
  }
]
```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: Using '*You was*' or using continuous with stative verbs ('*I was knowing the answer*')
- **Hindi Pattern**: हिन्दी में 'तुम सो रहे थे' में 'तुम' के साथ कभी-कभी एकवचन मानकर शिक्षार्थी 'You was' बोल देते हैं, या 'मुझे पता था' का अनुवाद '*I was knowing*' कर देते हैं।
- **Potential Interference**: `Saying '*You was going*' or '*I was wanting water*'.`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: 'You' ALWAYS takes 'were' in standard English. Stative verbs like 'know' and 'want' do not take progressive aspect.
- **Natural English Solution**: **Always say 'You were going' and 'I knew the answer / I wanted water'.**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day28_vocab_001` | **Cook** | Verb | `/kʊk/` | पकाना / खाना बनाना | She was cooking dinner when I called. <br> *जब मैंने फ़ोन किया, वह रात का खाना बना रही थी।* |
| `day28_vocab_002` | **Rain** | Verb/Noun | `/reɪn/` | बारिश होना | It was raining heavily at 6 AM. <br> *सुबह 6 बजे भारी बारिश हो रही थी।* |
| `day28_vocab_003` | **Drive** | Verb | `/draɪv/` | गाड़ी चलाना | He was driving to Delhi yesterday afternoon. <br> *वह कल दोपहर दिल्ली के लिए गाड़ी चला रहा था।* |
| `day28_vocab_004` | **Wait** | Verb | `/weɪt/` | इंतज़ार करना | We were waiting at the bus stop for an hour. <br> *हम एक घंटे से बस स्टॉप पर इंतज़ार कर रहे थे।* |
| `day28_vocab_005` | **Shine** | Verb | `/ʃaɪn/` | चमकना | The sun was shining brightly this morning. <br> *आज सुबह सूरज तेज़ी से चमक रहा था।* |
| `day28_vocab_006` | **Sleep** | Verb | `/sliːp/` | सोना | The baby was sleeping peacefully. <br> *बच्चा शांति से सो रहा था।* |
| `day28_vocab_007` | **Prepare** | Verb | `/prɪˈpeər/` | तैयारी करना | They were preparing for the final exams. <br> *वे अंतिम परीक्षाओं की तैयारी कर रहे थे।* |
| `day28_vocab_008` | **Listen** | Verb | `/ˈlɪs.ən/` | सुनना (ध्यान से) | I was listening to music with headphones. <br> *मैं हेडफ़ोन लगाकर संगीत सुन रहा था।* |
| `day28_vocab_009` | **Discuss** | Verb | `/dɪˈskʌs/` | चर्चा करना | The managers were discussing the new budget. <br> *प्रबंधक नए बजट पर चर्चा कर रहे थे।* |
| `day28_vocab_010` | **Travel** | Verb | `/ˈtræv.əl/` | यात्रा करना | We were traveling through the mountains. <br> *हम पहाड़ों से होकर यात्रा कर रहे थे।* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day28_phrase_001` [Conversational Inquiry]: **"What were you doing?"** — *आप क्या कर रहे थे?*
- `day28_phrase_002` [Friendly / Warm]: **"I was just thinking about you."** — *मैं बस तुम्हारे बारे में ही सोच रहा था।*
- `day28_phrase_003` [Descriptive]: **"It was raining outside."** — *बाहर बारिश हो रही थी।*
- `day28_phrase_004` [Descriptive]: **"They were having lunch."** — *वे दोपहर का भोजन कर रहे थे।*
- `day28_phrase_005` [Inquiry]: **"Were you sleeping?"** — *क्या तुम सो रहे थे?*
- `day28_phrase_006` [Time Expression]: **"At this exact time yesterday"** — *कल ठीक इसी समय*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day28_example_001`: **I was reading a book at 9 PM yesterday.**  
  *मैं कल रात 9 बजे एक किताब पढ़ रहा था।*
- `day28_example_002`: **She was cooking dinner for the family.**  
  *वह परिवार के लिए रात का खाना बना रही थी।*
- `day28_example_003`: **They were playing cricket in the park.**  
  *वे पार्क में क्रिकेट खेल रहे थे।*
- `day28_example_004`: **We were driving to Jaipur yesterday afternoon.**  
  *हम कल दोपहर जयपुर जा रहे थे (गाड़ी चला रहे थे)।*
- `day28_example_005`: **He wasn't paying attention to the teacher.**  
  *वह शिक्षक की बातों पर ध्यान नहीं दे रहा था।*
- `day28_example_006`: **Were you sleeping when the doorbell rang?**  
  *क्या तुम सो रहे थे जब दरवाज़े की घंटी बजी?*
- `day28_example_007`: **It was raining heavily throughout the night.**  
  *रात भर भारी बारिश हो रही थी।*
- `day28_example_008`: **What were you doing at 5 o'clock yesterday?**  
  *कल पाँच बजे आप क्या कर रहे थे?*
- `day28_example_009`: **The students were preparing for their annual test.**  
  *विद्यार्थी अपनी वार्षिक परीक्षा की तैयारी कर रहे थे।*
- `day28_example_010`: **I wasn't feeling well yesterday evening.**  
  *कल शाम मेरी तबीयत ठीक नहीं लग रही थी।*
- `day28_example_011`: **Why were they shouting outside the gate?**  
  *वे दरवाज़े के बाहर क्यों चिल्ला रहे थे?*
- `day28_example_012`: **She was listening to classical music in her room.**  
  *वह अपने कमरे में शास्त्रीय संगीत सुन रही थी।*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Weak forms of 'was' /wəz/ and 'were' /wə/ in unstressed positions, Stress on lexical verb-ing ('was RUN-ning', 'were TALK-ing')
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: In affirmative sentences, 'was' is pronounced /wəz/ (not a strong /wɒz/). 'Were' is pronounced /wə/ or /wər/. Keep them light and unstressed.
#### Audio Drills:
- `day28_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "I was reading a novel. /aɪ wəz ˈriːdɪŋ/"
- `day28_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "They were playing football. /ðeɪ wə ˈpleɪɪŋ/"
- `day28_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "What were you doing yesterday at eight?"
- `day28_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "She wasn't sleeping; she was working."

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (35 Items)
#### 1. `[day28_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PAST_CONTINUOUS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct sentence to say 'कल रात 8 बजे मैं टीवी देख रहा था':
**Options**: `I was watching TV at 8 PM yesterday.` | `I were watching TV at 8 PM yesterday.` | `I was watched TV at 8 PM yesterday.` | `I watched TV at 8 PM yesterday continuous.`
- **Correct Answer**: `I was watching TV at 8 PM yesterday.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Subject 'I' takes 'was' + Verb-ing ('watching').
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'I' के साथ 'was watching' आएगा।

#### 2. `[day28_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `SUBJECT_VERB_AGREEMENT`)
**Prompt**: Which auxiliary verb agrees with 'They' in past continuous?
**Options**: `were` | `was` | `is` | `are`
- **Correct Answer**: `were`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Plural subject 'They' takes 'were'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: बहुवचन कर्ता 'They' के साथ 'were' आता है।

#### 3. `[day28_ex_003]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `SUBJECT_VERB_AGREEMENT`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank with (was / were): 'Rahul and Aman ______ studying in the library.'
- **Correct Answer**: `were`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Compound plural subject ('Rahul and Aman') takes 'were'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: दो व्यक्तियों के नाम (बहुवचन) के साथ 'were' आएगा।

#### 4. `[day28_ex_004]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `PAST_CONTINUOUS`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank with the correct form of (sleep): 'She was ______ when I knocked on the door.'
- **Correct Answer**: `sleeping`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use the -ing form 'sleeping' after 'was'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Was' के बाद -ing रूप 'sleeping' आएगा।

#### 5. `[day28_ex_005]` [WORD_REORDER] (medium | `SENTENCE_BUILDING`)
**Prompt**: Arrange into a correct question: [you / doing / what / at 7 PM / were]
**Options**: `What were you doing at 7 PM?` | `What you were doing at 7 PM?` | `Were what you doing at 7 PM?` | `What doing were you at 7 PM?`
- **Correct Answer**: `What were you doing at 7 PM?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: What + were + you + doing + at 7 PM?
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: What were you doing at 7 PM?

#### 6. `[day28_ex_006]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_DETECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'You was driving too fast yesterday.'
- **Correct Answer**: `You were driving too fast yesterday.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'You' takes 'were', never 'was'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'You' के साथ 'were' आता है, 'was' नहीं।

#### 7. `[day28_ex_007]` [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (easy | `TRANSLATION_HI_EN`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'कल शाम बाहर बारिश हो रही थी।'
**Options**: `It was raining outside yesterday evening.` | `It were raining outside yesterday evening.` | `It was rain outside yesterday evening.` | `It rained outside yesterday evening continuous.`
- **Correct Answer**: `It was raining outside yesterday evening.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: It was raining outside yesterday evening.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अनुवाद: 'It was raining outside yesterday evening.'

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: Explaining to a friend why you couldn't pick up their phone calls yesterday afternoon.

#### Dialogue (`day28_dialogue_001`):
- **Alok**: "Hey Rohit, I called you three times yesterday around 3 PM. Where were you?"  
  *(अरे रोहित, मैंने कल दोपहर लगभग 3 बजे तुम्हें तीन बार फ़ोन किया। तुम कहाँ थे?)*
- **Rohit**: "I'm so sorry, Alok! At 3 PM, I was driving on the highway and it was raining heavily."  
  *(मुझे बहुत अफ़सोस है, आलोक! दोपहर 3 बजे मैं हाईवे पर गाड़ी चला रहा था और बहुत तेज़ बारिश हो रही थी।)*
- **Alok**: "Ah, I understand. Driving in heavy rain requires full concentration."  
  *(हाँ, मैं समझता हूँ। तेज़ बारिश में गाड़ी चलाने के लिए पूरा ध्यान चाहिए।)*
- **Rohit**: "Exactly. My phone was ringing in my bag, but I was focusing on the road."  
  *(बिल्कुल। मेरा फ़ोन बैग में बज रहा था, लेकिन मेरा ध्यान सड़क पर था।)*
- **Alok**: "No worries at all. What were you planning for this weekend?"  
  *(कोई बात नहीं। इस सप्ताहांत के लिए तुम्हारी क्या योजना थी?)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Describe what 3 different people in your home or office were doing yesterday at 6 PM using 'was/were + Verb-ing'.
- **HI**: कल शाम 6 बजे आपके घर या दफ़्तर में 3 लोग क्या कर रहे थे, इसका वर्णन 'was/were + V-ing' से करें।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day28_assess_001]` **What is the difference in meaning between 'I cooked dinner at 8 PM' and 'I was cooking dinner at 8 PM'?** ➔ *'I cooked dinner at 8 PM' means the cooking action started or completed as a whole event at 8 PM. 'I was cooking dinner at 8 PM' means the cooking was already in progress before 8 PM and continuing at that moment.* [Skill: Aspectual Discrimination]
- `[day28_assess_002]` **Which subjects take 'was' and which subjects take 'were'?** ➔ *'Was' is used with I, he, she, it, and singular nouns. 'Were' is used with you, we, they, and plural nouns.* [Skill: Subject Concord]
- `[day28_assess_003]` **Why cannot we say '*I was knowing the address*'?** ➔ *'Know' is a stative verb representing a cognitive state rather than a dynamic action; stative verbs do not take continuous aspect.* [Skill: Stative Verb Constraint]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `PAST_CONTINUOUS_BASICS`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14]
