## Day 29: Past Simple vs. Past Continuous with 'When' & 'While' (Interrupted Actions) {#day-29-day_29}
**शीर्षक**: Past Simple बनाम Past Continuous: 'When' और 'While' के साथ बाधित कार्य  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.2` | **Est. Duration**: 30 mins | **Prerequisites**: Day 21–28 (Past Simple & Past Continuous Full Paradigm)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Differentiate background ongoing actions (Past Continuous) from sudden interrupting actions (Past Simple).
  - **HI**: लंबे समय से चल रहे पृष्ठभूमि कार्य (Past Continuous) और बीच में अचानक घटित होने वाले बाधित कार्य (Past Simple) में अंतर करना।
- **EN**: Master the precise placement of subordinating conjunctions: 'while + Past Continuous' vs. 'when + Past Simple'.
  - **HI**: संयोजकों का सटीक प्रयोग: 'while + Past Continuous' (के दौरान) और 'when + Past Simple' (जब)।
- **EN**: Construct dual-action complex sentences (e.g., 'While I was cooking, the phone rang' / 'I was sleeping when the alarm went off').
  - **HI**: दोहरी क्रिया वाले जटिल वाक्य बनाना (जैसे: 'जब मैं खाना बना रहा था, तब फ़ोन बजा')।
- **EN**: Describe parallel past activities occurring simultaneously using 'While + Continuous, Continuous'.
  - **HI**: एक ही समय पर साथ-साथ चल रहे दो समानांतर कार्यों का वर्णन करना ('While he was studying, she was listening to music')।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
जब भूतकाल में कोई लंबा काम चल रहा था और उसी बीच अचानक कोई दूसरी छोटी घटना घट गई (बाधित कार्य / Interrupted Action), तो हम दोनों कालों को मिलाते हैं: 1. जो लंबा काम पहले से चल रहा था (Background Activity) ➔ Past Continuous (was/were + V-ing)। 2. जो घटना बीच में अचानक घटी (Short Interrupting Event) ➔ Past Simple (V2)। इनके साथ 'When' और 'While' का नियम बहुत आसान है: 'While' के तुरंत बाद आमतौर पर continuous आता है ('While I was cooking...'), और 'When' के तुरंत बाद sudden action (V2) आता है ('...when the phone rang')। अगर दोनों काम साथ-साथ चल रहे थे, तो दोनों में continuous आता है ('While I was reading, he was writing')।

**English Explanation**:
When narrating complex past scenarios, English pairs the Past Continuous (for the longer, ongoing background activity) with the Past Simple (for the shorter, punctual event that interrupts it). Subordinating Rules: 'While' introduces the continuous background action ('While + Past Continuous, [Past Simple]'). 'When' typically introduces the sudden interrupting action ('[Past Continuous] + when + [Past Simple]'). For two ongoing actions occurring simultaneously in parallel, 'While' can govern two continuous clauses ('While I was cooking, my sister was setting the table').

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: The Interrupted Action Rule (Long vs. Short Action)** [Aspectual Hierarchy]
  Long Background Action ➔ Past Continuous (was/were + V-ing) | Short Interrupting Event ➔ Past Simple (V2). Example: 'I was taking a shower (long) when the doorbell rang (short).'
- **Rule 2: The 'While' Subordination Pattern** [Subordinate Conjunction Syntax]
  While + [Past Continuous Clause], [Past Simple Clause]. Example: 'While they were driving home, it started to rain.' 'While' means 'during the time that'.
- **Rule 3: The 'When' Subordination Pattern** [Temporal Subordination]
  [Past Continuous Clause] + when + [Past Simple Clause]. Example: 'She was crossing the street when she dropped her phone.'
- **Rule 4: Simultaneous Parallel Past Actions** [Parallel Aspect]
  When two activities were in progress at the exact same time without interrupting each other: 'While mother was cooking, father was washing the dishes.'

#### Master Formula Table:
```json
[
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Interruption Pattern 1 (While)",
    "formula": "While + [Subject + was/were + V-ing], + [Subject + V2]",
    "exampleEn": "While I was studying, the electricity went off.",
    "meaningHi": "जब मैं पढ़ाई कर रहा था, तब बिजली चली गई।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Interruption Pattern 2 (When)",
    "formula": "[Subject + was/were + V-ing] + when + [Subject + V2]",
    "exampleEn": "I was sleeping when the alarm went off.",
    "meaningHi": "मैं सो रहा था जब अलार्म बजा।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Parallel Actions Pattern",
    "formula": "While + [Subject 1 + was/were + V-ing], + [Subject 2 + was/were + V-ing]",
    "exampleEn": "While Ravi was driving, Neha was navigating.",
    "meaningHi": "जब रवि गाड़ी चला रहा था, नेहा रास्ता देख रही थी।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Consecutive Actions Contrast",
    "formula": "[Subject + V2], then + [Subject + V2]",
    "exampleEn": "When the phone rang, I answered it.",
    "meaningHi": "जब फ़ोन बजा, मैंने उसे उठाया। (एक के बाद दूसरा)"
  }
]
```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: Using Past Simple for both actions ('*When the phone rang, I cooked*') or misplacing 'while' and 'when'
- **Hindi Pattern**: हिन्दी में 'जब फ़ोन बजा मैं खाना बना रहा था' को शिक्षार्थी कभी-कभी दोनों जगह Past Simple लगा देते हैं, जिससे अर्थ बदल जाता है कि फ़ोन बजने के बाद खाना बनाना शुरू किया।
- **Potential Interference**: `Saying '*While the phone rang, I was cooking*' or '*When I cooked, the phone rang*'.`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: 'While' marks the ongoing duration; 'when' marks the specific interrupting point in time.
- **Natural English Solution**: **Say 'While I was cooking, the phone rang' OR 'I was cooking when the phone rang'.**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day29_vocab_001` | **Interrupt** | Verb | `/ˌɪn.təˈrʌpt/` | बाधा डालना / बीच में टोकना | A loud knock interrupted our meeting. <br> *एक तेज़ दस्तक ने हमारी बैठक में बाधा डाली।* |
| `day29_vocab_002` | **Ring** | Verb | `/rɪŋ/` | बजना (घंटी/फ़ोन) | The telephone rang loudly three times. <br> *टेलीफ़ोन तीन बार तेज़ी से बजा।* |
| `day29_vocab_003` | **Drop** | Verb | `/drɒp/` | गिराना / गिर जाना | She dropped her purse while getting out of the taxi. <br> *टैक्सी से बाहर निकलते समय उसका पर्स गिर गया।* |
| `day29_vocab_004` | **Cross** | Verb | `/krɒs/` | पार करना (सड़क/पुल) | He saw an old friend while crossing the road. <br> *सड़क पार करते समय उसने एक पुराने दोस्त को देखा।* |
| `day29_vocab_005` | **Burn** | Verb | `/bɜːn/` | जलना / जल जाना | I burned the toast while I was answering the door. <br> *जब मैं दरवाज़ा खोलने गया, तब टोस्ट जल गया।* |
| `day29_vocab_006` | **Notice** | Verb | `/ˈnəʊ.tɪs/` | गौर करना / ध्यान में आना | I noticed a crack while I was cleaning the window. <br> *खिड़की साफ़ करते समय मुझे एक दरार दिखाई दी।* |
| `day29_vocab_007` | **Slip** | Verb | `/slɪp/` | फिसलना | He slipped on the wet floor while carrying the boxes. <br> *डिब्बे ले जाते समय वह गीले फ़र्श पर फिसल गया।* |
| `day29_vocab_008` | **Knock** | Verb/Noun | `/nɒk/` | खटखटाना / दस्तक | Someone knocked on the door while we were sleeping. <br> *जब हम सो रहे थे तब किसी ने दरवाज़ा खटखटाया।* |
| `day29_vocab_009` | **Break down** | Phrasal Verb | `/breɪk daʊn/` | खराब होना (वाहन/मशीन) | The car broke down while we were driving to Pune. <br> *जब हम पुणे जा रहे थे तब कार खराब हो गई।* |
| `day29_vocab_010` | **Arrive** | Verb | `/əˈraɪv/` | पहुँचना | The guests arrived while we were decorating the hall. <br> *मेहमान तब पहुँचे जब हम हॉल सजा रहे थे।* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day29_phrase_001` [Narrative clause]: **"While I was walking home"** — *जब मैं घर पैदल जा रहा था*
- `day29_phrase_002` [Temporal clause]: **"When the power went off"** — *जब बिजली चली गई*
- `day29_phrase_003` [Narrative]: **"The phone rang suddenly."** — *फ़ोन अचानक बज उठा।*
- `day29_phrase_004` [Conversational]: **"While you were away"** — *जब आप बाहर (अनुपस्थित) थे*
- `day29_phrase_005` [Idiomatic timing]: **"Just as I was leaving"** — *ठीक जब मैं निकल ही रहा था*
- `day29_phrase_006` [Narrative marker]: **"All of a sudden"** — *अचानक से*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day29_example_001`: **While I was cooking, the doorbell rang.**  
  *जब मैं खाना बना रहा था, तब दरवाज़े की घंटी बजी।*
- `day29_example_002`: **She was walking to the metro when she lost her wallet.**  
  *वह मेट्रो की ओर जा रही थी जब उसका बटुआ खो गया।*
- `day29_example_003`: **The electricity went off while we were watching the movie.**  
  *जब हम फ़िल्म देख रहे थे तब बिजली चली गई।*
- `day29_example_004`: **He was driving home when the tyre punctured.**  
  *वह घर की ओर गाड़ी चला रहा था जब टायर पंक्चर हो गया।*
- `day29_example_005`: **While Rohan was studying, his sister was listening to music.**  
  *जब रोहन पढ़ाई कर रहा था, उसकी बहन संगीत सुन रही थी।*
- `day29_example_006`: **I burned my finger while I was ironing the shirt.**  
  *शर्ट प्रेस करते समय मेरी उंगली जल गई।*
- `day29_example_007`: **We were having dinner when the guests arrived unexpectedly.**  
  *हम रात का खाना खा रहे थे जब मेहमान अचानक आ पहुँचे।*
- `day29_example_008`: **While he was waiting for the train, he read the newspaper.**  
  *जब वह ट्रेन का इंतज़ार कर रहा था, उसने अख़बार पढ़ा।*
- `day29_example_009`: **She slipped and fell while she was running for the bus.**  
  *बस पकड़ने के लिए दौड़ते समय वह फिसल कर गिर गई।*
- `day29_example_010`: **The teacher entered the classroom when the students were shouting.**  
  *जब छात्र चिल्ला रहे थे तब शिक्षक ने कक्षा में प्रवेश किया।*
- `day29_example_011`: **What were you doing when the earthquake happened?**  
  *जब भूकंप आया तब आप क्या कर रहे थे?*
- `day29_example_012`: **While I was cleaning the cupboard, I found my old school photos.**  
  *जब मैं अलमारी साफ़ कर रहा था, मुझे अपने स्कूल की पुरानी तस्वीरें मिलीं।*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Stress contrast: Ongoing background verb receives continuous rhythm, interrupting verb receives sharp peak stress, Pause at the comma boundary between clauses
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: Do not rush through the clause boundary. Insert a slight pitch-reset after the introductory clause: 'While I was COOK-ing, [pause] the phone RANG.'
#### Audio Drills:
- `day29_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "While I was studying, the lights went out."
- `day29_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "I was sleeping when the alarm rang."
- `day29_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "While she was cooking, he was washing the dishes."
- `day29_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "What were you doing when I called you?"

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (35 Items)
#### 1. `[day29_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `INTERRUPTED_ACTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct sentence to say 'जब मैं पढ़ाई कर रहा था, तब बिजली चली गई':
**Options**: `While I was studying, the electricity went off.` | `While I studied, the electricity was going off.` | `When I was study, the electricity went off.` | `While I was studying, the electricity was going off.`
- **Correct Answer**: `While I was studying, the electricity went off.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Ongoing action: 'was studying' + Interrupting action: 'went off'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: लंबा कार्य 'was studying' और बीच की घटना 'went off' है।

#### 2. `[day29_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `TEMPORAL_CONNECTORS`)
**Prompt**: Which conjunction usually introduces the ongoing continuous action?
**Options**: `While` | `When` | `Until` | `Before`
- **Correct Answer**: `While`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'While' is standardly paired with continuous aspect ('While I was driving').
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'While' के साथ Continuous Tense आता है।

#### 3. `[day29_ex_003]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `INTERRUPTED_ACTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank with the correct past form of (ring): 'I was having dinner when the phone ______.'
- **Correct Answer**: `rang`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use Past Simple 'rang' (V2) for the interrupting event.
- **Explanation (HI)**: बीच में घटने वाली क्रिया के लिए V2 'rang' आएगा।

#### 4. `[day29_ex_004]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `TEMPORAL_CONNECTORS`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank with (When / While): '______ I was walking in the park, I met an old school friend.'
- **Correct Answer**: `While`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `while`, `When`, `when`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'While' introduces the continuous background action ('was walking').
- **Explanation (HI)**: जारी कार्य के लिए 'While' सबसे उपयुक्त है।

#### 5. `[day29_ex_005]` [WORD_REORDER] (medium | `SENTENCE_BUILDING`)
**Prompt**: Arrange into a correct sentence: [the doorbell / was cooking / while / she / rang]
**Options**: `While she was cooking, the doorbell rang.` | `She was cooking while the doorbell rang.` | `While the doorbell rang, she was cooking.` | `Rang the doorbell while she was cooking.`
- **Correct Answer**: `While she was cooking, the doorbell rang.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: While + she was cooking (long), the doorbell rang (short).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: While she was cooking, the doorbell rang.

#### 6. `[day29_ex_006]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `ERROR_DETECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'I was sleeping when the alarm was ringing.'
- **Correct Answer**: `I was sleeping when the alarm rang.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Was ringing' should be Past Simple 'rang' for the sudden event after 'when'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'When' के बाद अचानक घटी घटना के लिए 'rang' आएगा।

#### 7. `[day29_ex_007]` [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (easy | `TRANSLATION_HI_EN`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'जब वह गाड़ी चला रहा था, तब उसका फ़ोन बजा।'
**Options**: `While he was driving, his phone rang.` | `When he was driving, his phone was ringing.` | `While he drove, his phone rang.` | `He was driving when his phone was ringing.`
- **Correct Answer**: `While he was driving, his phone rang.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: While he was driving, his phone rang.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अनुवाद: 'While he was driving, his phone rang.'

#### 8. `[day29_ex_008]` [ENGLISH_TO_HINDI] (easy | `TRANSLATION_EN_HI`)
**Prompt**: What is the Hindi meaning of: 'The car broke down while we were driving to Agra'?
**Options**: `जब हम आगरा जा रहे थे तब कार खराब हो गई।` | `कार खराब होने के बाद हम आगरा गए।` | `हम आगरा पहुँचे और कार खराब हो गई।` | `क्या आगरा जाते समय कार खराब हुई थी?`
- **Correct Answer**: `जब हम आगरा जा रहे थे तब कार खराब हो गई।`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'The car broke down while we were driving' means 'जब हम आगरा जा रहे थे तब कार खराब हो गई'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अर्थ: 'जब हम आगरा जा रहे थे तब कार खराब हो गई।'

#### 9. `[day29_ex_009]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (medium | `PARALLEL_ACTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Which sentence describes TWO simultaneous parallel actions?
**Options**: `While I was cooking, my brother was setting the table.` | `While I was cooking, the doorbell rang.` | `When I cooked, the doorbell rang.` | `I cooked dinner and then set the table.`
- **Correct Answer**: `While I was cooking, my brother was setting the table.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Both actions use Past Continuous ('was cooking' / 'was setting') to show parallel progress.
- **Explanation (HI)**: दोनों तरफ Continuous Tense होने से दोनों कार्य एक साथ चलने का बोध होता है।

#### 10. `[day29_ex_010]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `INTERRUPTED_ACTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank with the correct past form of (drop): 'She ______ her keys while she was opening her bag.'
- **Correct Answer**: `dropped`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Past Simple 'dropped' (short interrupting action).
- **Explanation (HI)**: अचानक घटी घटना के लिए 'dropped' आएगा।

#### 11. `[day29_ex_011]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `ERROR_DETECTION`)
**Prompt**: Correct the sentence: 'While the phone rang, I was taking a bath.'
- **Correct Answer**: `While I was taking a bath, the phone rang.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'While' must attach to the continuous background action ('taking a bath').
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'While' लंबे समय तक चलने वाले कार्य ('taking a bath') के साथ आता है।

#### 12. `[day29_ex_012]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PAST_QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct sentence to ask 'जब भूकंप आया तब तुम क्या कर रहे थे?':
**Options**: `What were you doing when the earthquake happened?` | `What did you do when the earthquake was happening?` | `What was you doing when the earthquake happened?` | `What were you do when the earthquake happened?`
- **Correct Answer**: `What were you doing when the earthquake happened?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: What were you doing (ongoing) when the earthquake happened (event)?
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही वाक्य: 'What were you doing when the earthquake happened?'

#### 13. `[day29_ex_013]` [WORD_REORDER] (medium | `SENTENCE_BUILDING`)
**Prompt**: Order: [when / was crossing / the road / he slipped / he]
**Options**: `He was crossing the road when he slipped.` | `He slipped when was crossing the road he.` | `When he was crossing the road he slipped.` | `He crossing the road was when he slipped.`
- **Correct Answer**: `He was crossing the road when he slipped.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Ongoing (was crossing) + when + sudden event (slipped).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: He was crossing the road when he slipped.

#### 14. `[day29_ex_014]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_DETECTION`)
**Prompt**: Fix the error: 'We was watching TV when someone knocked on the door.'
- **Correct Answer**: `We were watching TV when someone knocked on the door.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Change 'was' to 'were' with 'We'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'We' के साथ 'were' आएगा।

#### 15. `[day29_ex_015]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `INTERRUPTED_ACTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct sentence: 'सड़क पार करते समय उसने एक पर्स पाया':
**Options**: `While he was crossing the street, he found a purse.` | `When he was crossing the street, he was finding a purse.` | `While he crossed the street, he found a purse.` | `He found a purse when he crossed the street continuous.`
- **Correct Answer**: `While he was crossing the street, he found a purse.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: While + was crossing (long) + found (short).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही वाक्य: 'While he was crossing the street, he found a purse.'

#### 16. `[day29_ex_016]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `INTERRUPTED_ACTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank with the correct form of (burn): 'I was ironing my shirt when I ______ my finger.'
- **Correct Answer**: `burned`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `burnt`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Past Simple 'burned' or 'burnt'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: अचानक घटी घटना के लिए 'burned' या 'burnt' आएगा।

#### 17. `[day29_ex_017]` [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (easy | `TRANSLATION_HI_EN`)
**Prompt**: Translate: 'जब शिक्षक कक्षा में आए, छात्र बातें कर रहे थे।'
**Options**: `When the teacher entered the classroom, the students were talking.` | `When the teacher was entering the classroom, the students talked.` | `While the teacher entered, the students were talk.` | `The teacher entered when the students talked.`
- **Correct Answer**: `When the teacher entered the classroom, the students were talking.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: When the teacher entered (punctual), the students were talking (ongoing).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अनुवाद: 'When the teacher entered the classroom, the students were talking.'

#### 18. `[day29_ex_018]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (medium | `CONTRASTIVE_ASPECT`)
**Prompt**: Which pair shows the CORRECT contrast between ongoing and completed action?
**Options**: `I was reading (ongoing) / The light went out (interruption)` | `I read (ongoing) / The light was going out (interruption)` | `I was read (ongoing) / The light gone out (interruption)` | `I were reading (ongoing) / The light went out (interruption)`
- **Correct Answer**: `I was reading (ongoing) / The light went out (interruption)`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Was reading' is ongoing; 'went out' is punctual interruption.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Was reading' जारी कार्य और 'went out' बीच की घटना है।

#### 19. `[day29_ex_019]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `ERROR_DETECTION`)
**Prompt**: Fix the error: 'While I cooked, the phone was ringing.'
- **Correct Answer**: `While I was cooking, the phone rang.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Continuous on background action ('was cooking'), Past Simple on sudden event ('rang').
- **Explanation (HI)**: लंबे कार्य के लिए 'was cooking' और बजने के लिए 'rang' आएगा।

#### 20. `[day29_ex_020]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `PAST_CONTINUOUS`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank with the correct form of (watch): 'The power went off while we were ______ the cricket match.'
- **Correct Answer**: `watching`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Were watching' for ongoing activity.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Were' के बाद 'watching' आएगा।

#### 21. `[day29_ex_021]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `INTERRUPTED_ACTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Select the sentence with accurate grammatical subordination:
**Options**: `She fell asleep while she was reading the novel.` | `She was falling asleep while she read the novel.` | `She fell asleep while she reads the novel.` | `She was fell asleep when she read the novel.`
- **Correct Answer**: `She fell asleep while she was reading the novel.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Punctual event (fell asleep) during ongoing process (was reading).
- **Explanation (HI)**: पहला वाक्य पूर्णतः शुद्ध है।

#### 22. `[day29_ex_022]` [WORD_REORDER] (medium | `SENTENCE_BUILDING`)
**Prompt**: Order: [the guests / arrived / we were / having dinner / when]
**Options**: `We were having dinner when the guests arrived.` | `When the guests arrived we having dinner were.` | `We having dinner were when the guests arrived.` | `The guests arrived when having dinner we were.`
- **Correct Answer**: `We were having dinner when the guests arrived.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Ongoing (were having dinner) + when + sudden event (guests arrived).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: We were having dinner when the guests arrived.

#### 23. `[day29_ex_023]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `ERROR_DETECTION`)
**Prompt**: Correct the sentence: 'I was finding an old coin while I cleaned the garden.'
- **Correct Answer**: `I found an old coin while I was cleaning the garden.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Find' is punctual ('found'); 'clean' is ongoing ('was cleaning').
- **Explanation (HI)**: सिक्का मिलना अचानक हुआ ('found') और सफाई जारी थी ('was cleaning')।

#### 24. `[day29_ex_024]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `SUBJECT_VERB_AGREEMENT`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank: 'It started to rain while we ______ (was / were) walking home.'
- **Correct Answer**: `were`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Subject 'we' takes 'were'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'We' के साथ 'were' आएगा।

#### 25. `[day29_ex_025]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `INTERRUPTED_ACTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct sentence to say 'जब मैं नहा रहा था, तब किसी ने दरवाज़ा खटखटाया':
**Options**: `While I was taking a shower, someone knocked on the door.` | `When I took a shower, someone was knocking on the door.` | `While I took a shower, someone knocked on the door.` | `I was taking a shower while someone knocked on the door.`
- **Correct Answer**: `While I was taking a shower, someone knocked on the door.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: While + was taking a shower, someone knocked.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही वाक्य: 'While I was taking a shower, someone knocked on the door.'

#### 26. `[day29_ex_026]` [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (easy | `TRANSLATION_HI_EN`)
**Prompt**: Translate: 'जब मैं अलमारी साफ़ कर रहा था, मुझे मेरी पुरानी घड़ी मिली।'
**Options**: `While I was cleaning the cupboard, I found my old watch.` | `When I cleaned the cupboard, I was finding my old watch.` | `While I cleaned the cupboard, I found my old watch.` | `I was cleaning the cupboard when I was finding my old watch.`
- **Correct Answer**: `While I was cleaning the cupboard, I found my old watch.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: While I was cleaning the cupboard, I found my old watch.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अनुवाद: 'While I was cleaning the cupboard, I found my old watch.'

#### 27. `[day29_ex_027]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `ERROR_DETECTION`)
**Prompt**: Fix the error: 'When she was cooking, she was cutting her finger.'
- **Correct Answer**: `While she was cooking, she cut her finger.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Cutting a finger is an instant event ('cut', V2), not continuous.
- **Explanation (HI)**: उंगली कटना एक क्षणिक घटना है ('cut'), इसका continuous नहीं होगा।

#### 28. `[day29_ex_028]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `INTERRUPTED_ACTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank with the correct form of (see): 'While I was waiting at the bus stop, I ______ (see) an accident.'
- **Correct Answer**: `saw`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Past Simple 'saw' for the punctual event.
- **Explanation (HI)**: घटना देखने के लिए V2 'saw' आएगा।

#### 29. `[day29_ex_029]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (medium | `ASPECTUAL_DISCRIMINATION`)
**Prompt**: What is the difference between 'When the phone rang, I answered it' and 'When the phone rang, I was having dinner'?
**Options**: `The first is consecutive (one after another); the second is an interrupted ongoing activity.` | `Both mean the exact same thing.` | `The first is ongoing; the second is completed.` | `The second is in the future tense.`
- **Correct Answer**: `The first is consecutive (one after another); the second is an interrupted ongoing activity.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Answered it' happened after the ring; 'was having dinner' was already happening when the ring occurred.
- **Explanation (HI)**: पहला वाक्य एक के बाद दूसरी क्रिया और दूसरा वाक्य पहले से जारी कार्य को दर्शाता है।

#### 30. `[day29_ex_030]` [WORD_REORDER] (medium | `SENTENCE_BUILDING`)
**Prompt**: Reorder: [her bag / she was getting / dropped / while / she / off the train]
**Options**: `She dropped her bag while she was getting off the train.` | `While dropped she her bag she was getting off the train.` | `She was getting off the train she dropped her bag while.` | `Her bag she dropped while getting was off the train she.`
- **Correct Answer**: `She dropped her bag while she was getting off the train.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Punctual event (dropped her bag) + while + ongoing (was getting off).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: She dropped her bag while she was getting off the train.

#### 31. `[day29_ex_031]` [DIALOGUE_COMPLETION] (easy | `CONVERSATIONAL_USE`)
**Prompt**: Complete the dialogue: A: 'How did you hurt your knee?' B: 'I slipped while I ______ (was / were) running down the stairs.'
- **Correct Answer**: `was`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `was`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'I was running'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'I was running' आएगा।

#### 32. `[day29_ex_032]` [SPOKEN_RESPONSE] (easy | `SPEAKING`)
**Prompt**: Speak aloud: Describe an interrupted situation that happened to you using 'While I was..., [event happened]'.
- **Correct Answer**: `While I was [ongoing action], [sudden past action].`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Combine Past Continuous and Past Simple with 'While'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'While I was..., [घटना घटी]' बोलकर एक वाक्य बनाएं।

#### 33. `[day29_ex_033]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Which tense is used for the long background action that was already in progress?
- **Correct Answer**: `Past Continuous`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `past continuous`, `Past Progressive`, `past progressive`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Past Continuous (was/were + V-ing).
- **Explanation (HI)**: जारी रहने वाले कार्य के लिए Past Continuous का प्रयोग होता है।

#### 34. `[day29_ex_034]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Which tense is used for the short sudden event that interrupts the ongoing action?
- **Correct Answer**: `Past Simple`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `past simple`, `Simple Past`, `simple past`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Past Simple (V2).
- **Explanation (HI)**: अचानक बाधा डालने वाली घटना के लिए Past Simple (V2) का प्रयोग होता है।

#### 35. `[day29_ex_035]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Which word is typically followed by the continuous clause: 'While' or 'When'?
- **Correct Answer**: `While`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `while`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'While' is typically followed by the continuous clause.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'While' के बाद continuous clause आता है।

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: Reporting a minor kitchen accident to a doctor or family member during an evening catch-up.

#### Dialogue (`day29_dialogue_001`):
- **Doctor**: "Good evening, Mr. Sharma. How did you injure your left hand?"  
  *(शुभ संध्या, श्री शर्मा। आपके बाएँ हाथ में चोट कैसे लगी?)*
- **Mr. Sharma**: "Good evening, doctor. While I was chopping onions for dinner, the knife slipped."  
  *(शुभ संध्या, डॉक्टर। जब मैं डिनर के लिए प्याज काट रहा था, तब चाकू फिसल गया।)*
- **Doctor**: "I see. Were you distracted by something?"  
  *(अच्छा। क्या आपका ध्यान कहीं और भटक गया था?)*
- **Mr. Sharma**: "Yes, I was listening to the evening news on TV when the anchor shouted breaking news. That startled me."  
  *(हाँ, मैं टीवी पर शाम की ख़बरें सुन रहा था जब एंकर ने ब्रेकिंग न्यूज़ की घोषणा की। इससे मैं चौंक गया।)*
- **Doctor**: "Let me clean and dress the wound. It is a minor cut and will heal in two days."  
  *(मैं घाव को साफ़ करके पट्टी बाँध देता हूँ। यह एक छोटा सा कट है और दो दिनों में ठीक हो जाएगा।)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Narrate an incident where an activity was interrupted using 'While I was [action], [interruption happened]'.
- **HI**: 'While I was [जारी कार्य], [बाधा घटी]' का उपयोग करके किसी घटना का वर्णन करें।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day29_assess_001]` **How do you decide whether to use Past Simple or Past Continuous when two actions occur in the past?** ➔ *Use Past Continuous (was/were + V-ing) for the longer, background ongoing action, and Past Simple (V2) for the shorter, punctual event that interrupts it.* [Skill: Aspectual Hierarchy]
- `[day29_assess_002]` **Explain the functional difference between 'While I was cooking, the phone rang' and 'While I was cooking, my sister was baking'.** ➔ *The first represents an interrupted action (Continuous + Simple); the second represents two simultaneous parallel actions occurring at the same time (Continuous + Continuous).* [Skill: Subordination Precision]
- `[day29_assess_003]` **Why is 'While the phone rang, I was cooking' unidiomatic?** ➔ *'While' marks duration and should introduce the continuous action ('While I was cooking'), whereas 'when' marks the specific point in time when the short event occurred ('when the phone rang').* [Skill: Conjunction Syntax]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `PAST_SIMPLE_VS_CONTINUOUS_WHEN_WHILE`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14]

## Day 30: Stage 3 Cumulative Review & Past Narrative Mastery (Days 21–29) {#day-30-day_30}
**शीर्षक**: स्टेज 3 समग्र पुनरावलोकन एवं भूतकाल निपुणता (दिन 21–29)  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.2` | **Est. Duration**: 45 mins | **Prerequisites**: Days 21–29 (Complete Past Simple & Continuous Curriculum)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Synthesize all regular (-ed/-d/-ied) and high-frequency irregular past verb forms with zero phonological or spelling confusion.
  - **HI**: नियमित (-ed/-d/-ied) और अनियमित भूतकालीन क्रियाओं (V2) का बिना किसी वर्तनी या उच्चारण त्रुटि के सटीक उपयोग करना।
- **EN**: Eliminate all instances of the 'Double Past Error' across negative clauses ('didn't + V1') and interrogative clauses ('Did you + V1...?').
  - **HI**: नकारात्मक ('didn't + V1') और प्रश्नवाचक ('Did you + V1...?') वाक्यों में 'Double Past Error' को पूरी तरह समाप्त करना।
- **EN**: Construct rich, coherent multi-clause past narratives using temporal markers ('ago', 'last', 'yesterday') and logical connectors ('First', 'Then', 'Because', 'So', 'That's why').
  - **HI**: समय संकेतकों ('ago', 'last', 'yesterday') और संयोजकों ('First', 'Then', 'Because', 'So', 'That's why') का उपयोग करके विस्तृत कहानियाँ और घटनाक्रम सुनाना।
- **EN**: Master the interplay of ongoing background actions (Past Continuous: was/were + V-ing) and sudden interruptions (Past Simple: V2) with 'When' and 'While'.
  - **HI**: लंबे समय से चल रहे कार्यों (Past Continuous: was/were + V-ing) और बीच में घटी घटनाओं (Past Simple: V2) को 'When' और 'While' के साथ सटीकता से जोड़ना।
- **EN**: Demonstrate complete communicative fluency in narrating past experiences, explaining causes and effects, and asking conversational inquiries.
  - **HI**: भूतकाल की घटनाओं को सुनाने, कारण व परिणाम बताने और दूसरों से प्रश्न पूछने में पूर्ण आत्मविश्वास और प्रवाह हासिल करना।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
बधाई हो! आज दिन 30 है—यह स्टेज 3 (Days 21–29) का समग्र पुनरावलोकन (Mastery Review) है। इस स्टेज में आपने भूतकाल की पूरी दुनिया सीखी है: 
1. Regular Verbs (played, watched, studied) और Irregular Verbs (went, saw, bought, took)।
2. Negative sentences का नियम: 'didn't + V1' (जैसे: 'I didn't go', '*didn't went*' कभी नहीं)।
3. Yes/No व WH- प्रश्न: 'Did you see...?' और 'Where did you go...?' (यहाँ भी 'did' के बाद V1 आता है)।
4. समय और क्रम: 'two days ago', 'last night', 'First', 'Then', 'After that', 'Finally'।
5. कारण और परिणाम: 'Because' (कारण) और 'So / That's why' (परिणाम)—दोनों को एक साथ कभी नहीं मिलाना है।
6. Past Continuous (रहा था / रही थी): 'was / were + V-ing'।
7. Interrupted Actions: जब कोई काम चल रहा था (While I was cooking) और बीच में कोई घटना घटी (the phone rang)।
आज का अभ्यास इन सभी नियमों को जोड़कर आपको एक धाराप्रवाह कथावाचक (Storyteller) बनाएगा!

**English Explanation**:
Welcome to Day 30—the comprehensive consolidation and mastery review for Stage 3 (Days 21–29). In this stage, you transformed from basic present-tense descriptions into a complete English storyteller capable of narrating multifaceted past events. The master past architecture encompasses: 1) Affirmative V2 inflection (Regular -ed vs. High-frequency Irregular ablauts); 2) The auxiliary operator DID in Negatives ('didn't + V1') and Questions ('Did you / Where did you + V1...?'); 3) Temporal anchoring and narrative cohesion ('ago', 'last night', 'First', 'Then', 'Finally'); 4) Causal and consecutive reasoning ('because' vs. 'so / that's why'); and 5) Aspectual contrast between ongoing background durations (Past Continuous: was/were + V-ing) and punctual interruptions (Past Simple: V2) governed by 'While' and 'When'.

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: The Universal Master Rule of Past Simple (V2 vs. V1)** [Core Syntax Pillar]
  Affirmative statements require V2 (walked, went). Negatives and Questions REQUIRE auxiliary 'did/didn't' with Base Verb V1 (didn't go, did you see). The Double Past (*didn't went*, *did you saw*) is strictly forbidden.
- **Rule 2: Temporal Anchor Prepositionless Collocations** [Idiomatic Precision]
  Duration + ago ('two days ago'). Last + period ('last night', 'last week'). Yesterday + morning/afternoon/evening. No prepositions precede 'last' or 'yesterday'.
- **Rule 3: Causal vs. Result Clause Independence** [Discourse Logic]
  Use 'because' for causes (Result because Reason) and 'so' for results (Reason, so Result). Never combine '*Because... so...*' in the same sentence.
- **Rule 4: Aspectual Hierarchy (Continuous Background vs. Punctual Event)** [Aspectual Harmony]
  Ongoing activity ➔ was/were + V-ing (While I was driving). Sudden event ➔ V2 (when the tyre burst). Parallel actions ➔ While A was doing X, B was doing Y.

#### Master Formula Table:
```json
[
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Past Simple Affirmative",
    "formula": "Subject + V2 (regular -ed / irregular) + [Object]",
    "exampleEn": "She bought a new laptop yesterday.",
    "meaningHi": "उसने कल एक नया लैपटॉप खरीदा।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Past Simple Negative",
    "formula": "Subject + didn't + Base Verb (V1) + [Object]",
    "exampleEn": "We didn't miss the morning flight.",
    "meaningHi": "हमारी सुबह की फ़्लाइट नहीं छूटी।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Past Simple Inquiries",
    "formula": "(WH-) + did + Subject + Base Verb (V1)...?",
    "exampleEn": "Where did you stay in Mumbai?",
    "meaningHi": "आप मुंबई में कहाँ रुके थे?"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Interrupted Aspect (When/While)",
    "formula": "While + [was/were + V-ing], + [Subject + V2]",
    "exampleEn": "While I was studying, the lights went out.",
    "meaningHi": "जब मैं पढ़ाई कर रहा था, तब बत्तियाँ बुझ गईं।"
  }
]
```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: Cumulative Diagnostic: Top 4 Stage-3 Traps
- **Hindi Pattern**: 1. '*didn't went*' (Double Past)
2. '*before two days*' instead of 'two days ago'
3. '*Because I was sick, so I stayed home*' (Double Conjunction)
4. '*You was going*' instead of 'You were going'.
- **Potential Interference**: `Carrying these four systemic errors into extended conversational storytelling.`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: Each of these stems from direct mother-tongue translation without observing English syntactic constraints.
- **Natural English Solution**: **1. 'didn't go' | 2. 'two days ago' | 3. 'Because I was sick, I stayed home' | 4. 'You were going'.**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day30_vocab_001` | **Experience** | Noun/Verb | `/ɪkˈspɪə.ri.əns/` | अनुभव / अनुभव करना | We experienced heavy traffic on our way. <br> *रास्ते में हमें भारी ट्रैफ़िक का सामना करना पड़ा।* |
| `day30_vocab_002` | **Accidentally** | Adverb | `/ˌæk.sɪˈden.təl.i/` | गलती से / अनजाने में | I accidentally deleted the important email. <br> *मैंने गलती से वह ज़रूरी ईमेल डिलीट कर दिया।* |
| `day30_vocab_003` | **Immediately** | Adverb | `/ɪˈmiː.di.ət.li/` | तुरंत / फौरन | He immediately called the customer support. <br> *उसने तुरंत कस्टमर सपोर्ट को फ़ोन किया।* |
| `day30_vocab_004` | **Realize** | Verb | `/ˈrɪə.laɪz/` | एहसास होना / समझ में आना | I realized my mistake after reaching home. <br> *घर पहुँचने के बाद मुझे अपनी गलती का एहसास हुआ।* |
| `day30_vocab_005` | **Fortunately** | Adverb | `/ˈfɔː.tʃən.ət.li/` | सौभाग्य से | Fortunately, nobody was injured in the incident. <br> *सौभाग्य से, इस घटना में कोई घायल नहीं हुआ।* |
| `day30_vocab_006` | **Unfortunately** | Adverb | `/ʌnˈfɔː.tʃən.ət.li/` | दुर्भाग्य से | Unfortunately, the store was closed yesterday. <br> *दुर्भाग्य से, कल दुकान बंद थी।* |
| `day30_vocab_007` | **Suddenly** | Adverb | `/ˈsʌd.ən.li/` | अचानक | Suddenly, it started raining heavily. <br> *अचानक भारी बारिश होने लगी।* |
| `day30_vocab_008` | **Remember** | Verb | `/rɪˈmem.bər/` | याद आना / याद रखना | I remembered her birthday at the last moment. <br> *मुझे अंतिम समय पर उसका जन्मदिन याद आया।* |
| `day30_vocab_009` | **Decide** | Verb | `/dɪˈsaɪd/` | निर्णय लेना / फ़ैसला करना | They decided to travel by train. <br> *उन्होंने ट्रेन से यात्रा करने का फ़ैसला किया।* |
| `day30_vocab_010` | **Celebrate** | Verb | `/ˈsel.ə.breɪt/` | उत्सव मनाना / जश्न मनाना | We celebrated our success last weekend. <br> *हमने पिछले सप्ताहांत अपनी सफलता का जश्न मनाया।* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day30_phrase_001` [Narrative Idiom]: **"To cut a long story short"** — *संक्षेप में कहें तो*
- `day30_phrase_002` [Temporal Transition]: **"As soon as I reached"** — *जैसे ही मैं पहुँचा*
- `day30_phrase_003` [Narrative Marker]: **"All of a sudden"** — *अचानक से*
- `day30_phrase_004` [Conversational]: **"That reminds me of yesterday."** — *इससे मुझे कल की बात याद आ गई।*
- `day30_phrase_005` [Conversational Idiom]: **"Believe it or not"** — *मानो या न मानो*
- `day30_phrase_006` [Resolution]: **"In the end, everything was fine."** — *अंत में, सब कुछ ठीक रहा।*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day30_example_001`: **I woke up early yesterday, had coffee, and went for a brisk walk.**  
  *मैं कल सुबह जल्दी उठा, कॉफ़ी पी और तेज़ सैर के लिए गया।*
- `day30_example_002`: **She didn't receive the package because the delivery person lost the address.**  
  *उसे पार्सल नहीं मिला क्योंकि डिलीवरी वाले से पता खो गया था।*
- `day30_example_003`: **Where did you buy this beautiful handmade carpet?**  
  *आपने यह सुंदर हस्तनिर्मित कालीन कहाँ से खरीदी?*
- `day30_example_004`: **While we were having a meeting, the fire alarm went off.**  
  *जब हम बैठक कर रहे थे, तब फ़ायर अलार्म बज उठा।*
- `day30_example_005`: **First, I completed my chores. Then, I watched my favorite documentary.**  
  *पहले मैंने अपने घरेलू काम निपटाए। फिर मैंने अपनी पसंदीदा डॉक्यूमेंट्री देखी।*
- `day30_example_006`: **He was very tired, so he went to bed at nine o'clock.**  
  *वह बहुत थका हुआ था, इसलिए वह नौ बजे सोने चला गया।*
- `day30_example_007`: **Did you talk to the manager about your leave application?**  
  *क्या आपने अपनी छुट्टी के आवेदन के बारे में मैनेजर से बात की?*
- `day30_example_008`: **Three days ago, we visited our grandparents in Chandigarh.**  
  *तीन दिन पहले हम चंडीगढ़ में अपने दादा-दादी से मिलने गए थे।*
- `day30_example_009`: **What were you doing yesterday at four in the afternoon?**  
  *कल दोपहर चार बजे आप क्या कर रहे थे?*
- `day30_example_010`: **She dropped her glasses while she was cleaning the bookshelf.**  
  *किताबों की अलमारी साफ़ करते समय उसका चश्मा गिर गया।*
- `day30_example_011`: **Because the road was blocked, we took the expressway.**  
  *क्योंकि रास्ता बंद था, हमने एक्सप्रेसवे का रास्ता लिया।*
- `day30_example_012`: **Finally, after searching for two hours, I found my car keys.**  
  *आखिरकार दो घंटे ढूँढने के बाद मुझे अपनी कार की चाबियाँ मिल गईं।*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Mastery of all 3 regular -ed endings: /t/, /d/, and /ɪd/, Prosody and rhythm in long multi-clause narrative sentences
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: Do not let connected speech break down during complex compound sentences. Maintain smooth breathing, linking, and intonational stepping.
#### Audio Drills:
- `day30_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "Walked /t/, Cleaned /d/, Decided /ɪd/."
- `day30_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "I didn't go to the market yesterday."
- `day30_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "While I was studying, my phone rang."
- `day30_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "First we had tea, then we left the house."

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (50 Items)
#### 1. `[day30_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `REGULAR_PAST`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct past simple sentence with regular verb:
**Options**: `She visited her grandmother last Sunday.` | `She visit her grandmother last Sunday.` | `She visiteded her grandmother last Sunday.` | `She was visit her grandmother last Sunday.`
- **Correct Answer**: `She visited her grandmother last Sunday.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Regular past: visit + ed = visited.
- **Explanation (HI)**: नियमित क्रिया: 'visited' सही है।

#### 2. `[day30_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `IRREGULAR_PAST`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct irregular past form of (buy):
**Options**: `bought` | `buyed` | `boughted` | `buyen`
- **Correct Answer**: `bought`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Irregular past of buy is 'bought'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Buy' का V2 रूप 'bought' होता है।

#### 3. `[day30_ex_003]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `PAST_NEGATIVE`)
**Prompt**: Fix the Double Past mistake: 'I didn't went to the office yesterday.'
- **Correct Answer**: `I didn't go to the office yesterday.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Didn't' must be followed by base verb 'go' (V1).
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Didn't' के बाद 'go' (V1) आएगा।

#### 4. `[day30_ex_004]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PAST_QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct Yes/No past question:
**Options**: `Did you see the news yesterday?` | `Did you saw the news yesterday?` | `Do you saw the news yesterday?` | `Were you see the news yesterday?`
- **Correct Answer**: `Did you see the news yesterday?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Did' + Subject + Base verb 'see' (V1).
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Did you see' सही प्रश्न है।

#### 5. `[day30_ex_005]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `TIME_MARKERS`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank with the correct time marker (ago / before): 'I completed my college degree two years ______.'
- **Correct Answer**: `ago`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Duration (two years) + 'ago'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: समय अवधि के बाद 'ago' आता है।

#### 6. `[day30_ex_006]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `TIME_MARKERS`)
**Prompt**: Correct the collocation: 'We watched a cricket match yesterday night.'
- **Correct Answer**: `We watched a cricket match last night.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Last night' is the standard expression for 'कल रात'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Yesterday night' की जगह 'last night' आएगा।

#### 7. `[day30_ex_007]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `CAUSAL_CONNECTORS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the sentence that correctly gives a reason with 'because':
**Options**: `I stayed home because I had a headache.` | `Because I had a headache, so I stayed home.` | `I stayed home so I had a headache.` | `I had a headache because I stayed home.`
- **Correct Answer**: `I stayed home because I had a headache.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Single causal conjunction 'because' without 'so'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Because' के साथ 'so' का प्रयोग नहीं होता।

#### 8. `[day30_ex_008]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `RESULT_CONNECTORS`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank with (because / so): 'The weather was terrible, ______ we canceled our road trip.'
- **Correct Answer**: `so`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'We canceled our road trip' is the result.
- **Explanation (HI)**: रोड ट्रिप रद्द करना परिणाम था, इसलिए 'so' आएगा।

#### 9. `[day30_ex_009]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PAST_CONTINUOUS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct Past Continuous sentence:
**Options**: `They were playing chess at 8 PM.` | `They was playing chess at 8 PM.` | `They were played chess at 8 PM.` | `They did playing chess at 8 PM.`
- **Correct Answer**: `They were playing chess at 8 PM.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Plural subject 'They' + were + playing.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'They' के साथ 'were playing' आएगा।

#### 10. `[day30_ex_010]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (medium | `INTERRUPTED_ACTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Which sentence accurately represents an interrupted action?
**Options**: `While I was cooking, the telephone rang.` | `When I was cooking, the telephone was ringing.` | `While I cooked, the telephone rang.` | `I cooked when the telephone was ringing.`
- **Correct Answer**: `While I was cooking, the telephone rang.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Continuous background ('was cooking') + punctual event ('rang').
- **Explanation (HI)**: लंबा कार्य 'was cooking' और बीच की घटना 'rang' है।

#### 11. `[day30_ex_011]` [WORD_REORDER] (easy | `SEQUENCING_CONNECTORS`)
**Prompt**: Arrange into a correct past narrative: [tea / we / then / first / had / left / we]
**Options**: `First we had tea, then we left.` | `Then we left, first we had tea.` | `We had tea first, left then we.` | `First left we, then had we tea.`
- **Correct Answer**: `First we had tea, then we left.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: First [Action 1], then [Action 2].
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: First we had tea, then we left.

#### 12. `[day30_ex_012]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `WH_PAST_QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Fix the WH- question error: 'Where you went yesterday evening?'
- **Correct Answer**: `Where did you go yesterday evening?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Insert auxiliary 'did' and change 'went' to base verb 'go'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Where did you go yesterday evening?' सही रूप है।

#### 13. `[day30_ex_013]` [FILL_IN_BLANK] (easy | `IRREGULAR_PAST`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank with the V2 form of (write): 'She ______ a long email to her manager.'
- **Correct Answer**: `wrote`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Past tense of write is 'wrote'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Write' का V2 'wrote' होगा।

#### 14. `[day30_ex_014]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `SHORT_ANSWERS`)
**Prompt**: What is the correct short answer to: 'Did they win the championship?'
**Options**: `Yes, they did.` | `Yes, they won.` | `Yes, they do.` | `Yes, they did won.`
- **Correct Answer**: `Yes, they did.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Formulaic short answer: 'Yes, they did.'
- **Explanation (HI)**: संक्षिप्त उत्तर: 'Yes, they did.'

#### 15. `[day30_ex_015]` [HINDI_TO_ENGLISH] (medium | `TRANSLATION_HI_EN`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'जब मैं सड़क पार कर रहा था, मैंने एक पुराना दोस्त देखा।'
**Options**: `While I was crossing the road, I saw an old friend.` | `When I crossed the road, I was seeing an old friend.` | `While I crossed the road, I saw an old friend.` | `I was crossing the road when I was seeing an old friend.`
- **Correct Answer**: `While I was crossing the road, I saw an old friend.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: While I was crossing (ongoing) + saw (punctual).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अनुवाद: 'While I was crossing the road, I saw an old friend.'

#### 16. `[day30_ex_016]` [ENGLISH_TO_HINDI] (easy | `TRANSLATION_EN_HI`)
**Prompt**: What is the Hindi meaning of: 'I didn't attend the meeting because I was unwell'?
**Options**: `मैं बैठक में शामिल नहीं हुआ क्योंकि मेरी तबीयत ठीक नहीं थी।` | `मैं बैठक में था क्योंकि मैं ठीक था।` | `मैं बीमार था इसलिए बैठक में गया।` | `क्या आप बैठक में शामिल हुए थे?`
- **Correct Answer**: `मैं बैठक में शामिल नहीं हुआ क्योंकि मेरी तबीयत ठीक नहीं थी।`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'I didn't attend the meeting because I was unwell' means 'मैं बैठक में शामिल नहीं हुआ क्योंकि मेरी तबीयत ठीक नहीं थी'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही अर्थ: 'मैं बैठक में शामिल नहीं हुआ क्योंकि मेरी तबीयत ठीक नहीं थी।'

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: A job candidate answering the classic behavioral interview question: 'Tell me about a difficult problem you encountered at work and how you resolved it.'

#### Dialogue (`day30_dialogue_001`):
- **Interviewer**: "Mr. Kapoor, can you tell me about an unexpected challenge you faced in your previous role?"  
  *(श्री कपूर, क्या आप पिछली नौकरी में आई किसी अप्रत्याशित चुनौती के बारे में बता सकते हैं?)*
- **Rohan Kapoor**: "Certainly. Last month, while we were preparing for a major client demo, our primary cloud server crashed unexpectedly."  
  *(ज़रूर। पिछले महीने, जब हम एक बड़े क्लाइंट डेमो की तैयारी कर रहे थे, तब हमारा मुख्य क्लाउड सर्वर अचानक क्रैश हो गया।)*
- **Interviewer**: "That must have been stressful. What did you do immediately?"  
  *(वह काफी तनावपूर्ण रहा होगा। आपने तुरंत क्या किया?)*
- **Rohan Kapoor**: "First, I informed the client about a short 15-minute delay. Then, I worked with the DevOps engineer to restore the backup database."  
  *(पहले, मैंने क्लाइंट को 15 मिनट की छोटी देरी के बारे में सूचित किया। फिर, मैंने बैकअप डेटाबेस को रीस्टोर करने के लिए डेवऑप्स इंजीनियर के साथ काम किया।)*
- **Interviewer**: "Did the demo succeed in the end?"  
  *(क्या अंत में डेमो सफल रहा?)*
- **Rohan Kapoor**: "Yes, it did! We delivered a smooth presentation, and the client signed the contract two days later because they appreciated our transparency and quick recovery."  
  *(हाँ, बिल्कुल! हमने एक शानदार प्रेजेंटेशन दिया, और क्लाइंट ने दो दिन बाद अनुबंध पर हस्ताक्षर किए क्योंकि उन्होंने हमारी पारदर्शिता और त्वरित समाधान की सराहना की।)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Deliver a 1-minute past story about a challenge you solved in the past using: 'Last month/year...', 'While I was...', 'First... Then...', 'Because... so...', 'Finally...'.
- **HI**: भूतकाल की किसी चुनौती और उसके समाधान पर स्टेज 3 के सभी कनेक्टर्स का उपयोग करके 1 मिनट का भाषण दें।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day30_assess_001]` **Summarize the 4 most critical structural rules mastered in Stage 3 (Days 21–29).** ➔ *1. V2 in past affirmative vs. Base V1 with 'didn't' and 'did'.
2. Postpositional 'ago' and prepositionless 'last night'.
3. Single causal conjunction ('because' OR 'so', never both together).
4. Aspectual contrast: Past Continuous for background duration with 'while' and Past Simple for sudden interruptions with 'when'.* [Skill: Curricular Mastery]
- `[day30_assess_002]` **How has your ability to communicate in English evolved from Stage 1 (Present Simple) to Stage 3 (Past Narrative)?** ➔ *Stage 1 established present routines and identities ('I work', 'She is a doctor'); Stage 3 unlocks complete narrative storytelling, historical explanation, chronological reporting, and multi-clause cause-and-effect reasoning in past time.* [Skill: Metacognitive Fluency]
- `[day30_assess_003]` **What is your remediation strategy if you catch yourself saying '*I didn't went* '?** ➔ *Immediately self-correct: pause, replace the V2 with base V1 ('I didn't go'), and reinforce the rule that the auxiliary 'didn't' already absorbed the past marker.* [Skill: Self-Correction Competence]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `STAGE_3_CUMULATIVE_PAST_MASTERY`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14, 30]
